paṇṭit tamiḻaka varalāṟu
kiṟittavamum tamiḻum

iḷaṅkaṇi patippakam

patippuri
19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyum, 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkap pakutiyum tamiḻukkum, tamiḻiṉattiṟkum pukaḻpūtta poṟ kālamākum.  tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ mīṭṭuruvākkattiṟkum, tamiḻiṉa mīṭcikkum vittūṉṟiya kālam.  tamiḻmaṟumalarcci varalāṟṟil ōr ellik kal.
ikkālac cūḻaliltāṉ tamiḻiyum, tamiḻiṉattiyum uyirāka vum mūccākavum koṇṭa aruntamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷum, talivarkaḷum tōṉṟi moḻikkum, iṉattiṟkum perum paṅkāṟṟiṉar. ip poṟkālattiltāṉ aṟiñar mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ 6.12.1900il tōṉṟi 8.5.1980il maṟintār.  vāḻnta kālam 80 āṇṭukaḷ.  tirumaṇam ceyyāmal tuṟavu vāḻkki mēṟkoṇṭu tamiḻ muṉivarāka vāḻntu ari nūṟṟāṇṭukku mēl ariya tamiḻp paṇi ceytu maṟintavar.  tamiḻkūṟum nallulakam vaṇaṅkattakkavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittum tamiḻiṉam taṉṉi uṇarvataṟkum, talinimirvataṟkum, āyvāḷarkaḷ āyvup paṇiyil mēlāyvi mēṟkoḷvataṟkum vaḻikāṭṭuvaṉavākum.  āyvunōkkil virinta pārviyuṭaṉ tamiḻukku aḻiyāta aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi vippāka vittuc ceṉṟavar.  tamiḻ - tamiḻariṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi mīṭṭeṭuttuttanta toltamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ variciyil kuṟippiṭattakkavar.  tamiḻmaṇṇil 1937-1938il naṭanta mutal inti etirppup pōri muṉṉeṭuttucceṉṟa talivarkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ variciyil ivarum oruvar. varalāṟu, ilakkiyam, kali, camayam toṭarpāṉa āyvu nūlkaḷiyum, potunalaṉ toṭarpāṉa nūlkaḷiyum, paṉmukap pārviyuṭaṉ eḻutiyavar.  
pērāciriyar muṉivar vī. aracu avarkaḷ eḻutiya cākittiya akātemi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa ‘intiya ilakkiyac ciṟpikaḷ’  nūlvariciyil mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi paṟṟiya varalāṟṟu nūlil āvaṇappaṇi, varalāṟu eḻutu paṇi, kali varalāṟu, karuttu nili ākiya poruḷkaḷil ivartam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipula aṟiviṉi mika āḻamākap pativuceytuḷḷār.
‘muṟiyāṉa tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟi iṉi eḻutuvataṟku etirkāla āyvāḷarkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭic ceṉṟavar’ - eṉpār kā. cuppiramaṇiyapiḷḷi avarkaḷ. ‘mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi āṇṭil iḷiyavarāka iruntālum, ārāyccit tuṟiyil mutiyavar, nalloḻukkam vāyntavar. nallōr kūṭṭuṟavip poṉṉē pōl pōṟṟiyavar.’ eṉṟu cuvāmi vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār avarkaḷum, “eṇpatāṇṭu vāḻntu, taṉip perum tuṟavupūṇṭu, piṟar pukāta āyvuccūḻalil pukuntu tamiḻ vaḷartta, ulakac camayaṅkaḷiyum, kalveṭṭu kāṭṭum varalāṟukaḷiyum, ciṟpam uṇarttum kalikaḷiyum tōyntu āyntu tōlā nūlkaḷ eḻutiya ārāycci aṟiñar mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaṭkut tamiḻp pēravic cemmal eṉṟa paṭṭattiṉi vaḻaṅkiyum, tamiḻc cemmalkaḷ pēraviyiṉ ōr uṟuppiṉarāka ēṟṟukkoṇṭum, maturik kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakam pārāṭṭic ciṟappikkiṟatu” eṉṟu  ip peruntamiḻ aṟiñari appalkalik kaḻakam pōṟṟiyuḷḷati maṉattil koṇṭu ivariṉ aṉittup paṭippukaḷiyum veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟōm.
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka tamiḻ ilakkiyattuṟit talivar
vī. aracu avarkaḷ - pōṟṟutalukkum, pukaḻukkum uriya ivvārāyccip pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum poruḷvaḻip pirittu,  eṅkaḷukkuk koṭuttu utaviyatuṭaṉ, patippāciriyarāka iruntum, vaḻikāṭṭiyum, ivvārāyccit tokutikaḷi āyvāḷarkaḷum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷum ciṟanta payaṉpeṟum nōkkil veḷiyiṭuvataṟku pallāṟṟāṉum utaviṉār.   avarukku em naṉṟi.  ivvaruntamiḻc celvaṅkaḷi aṉivarum vāṅkip payaṉaṭiya vēṇṭukiṟōm.
ivvārāycci nūlkaḷ ellā vakiyilum ciṟappōṭu veḷi varuvataṟku utaviya aṉivarkkum naṉṟi.
- patippāḷar


tamiḻ ilakkiyavaralāṟu
 kiṟittavamum tamiḻum
1936 ām āṇṭil mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ eḻutiya mutal nūl kiṟittavamum tamiḻum eṉum innūlākum. ‘kiṟittavarāl tamiḻmoḻikku uṇṭāṉa naṉmikaḷik kūṟum nūl’ eṉpatu innūlukku ivar koṭuttuḷḷa tuṇit talippu ākum. airōppiyarkaḷ varukiyōṭu kiṟittuvam tamiḻnāṭṭil evvakiyāṉa  paṇpāṭṭut tākkaṅkaḷi uruvākkiyatu eṉpatē innūliṉ pāṭuporuḷāka amikiṟatu. tamiḻccūḻalil ceyyuḷ marapē perum vaḻakkāka iruntapōtu ammarapiṟku iṇiyāṉa urinaṭi vaṭivatti kālaṉiya celvākkē namakku uruvākkiyatu. itil kiṟittuva pātiriyārkaḷiṉ paṅkaḷippu mutaṉmiyāṉatu. ittaṉmi kuṟittu virivāṉa pativi innūlil mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. ceyyuḷ naṭiyil eḻutappaṭum nūlkaḷ orucilarāl maṭṭumē vācikkappaṭum cūḻal iruntatu. āṉāl uri naṭiyil eḻutappaṭum nūlkaḷip paravalāka eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟavarkaḷ aṉivarum vācikkamuṭintatu. ittaṉmi kiṟittuva pātiriyārkaḷāl evvakiyil paravalākkappaṭṭatu eṉpati mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷār.
ōliccuvaṭiyil irunta namatu nūlkaḷ accuvaṭivam peṟṟataṉ varalāṟṟiyum ataṉmūlam ēṟpaṭṭa tākkaṅkaḷiyum innūlil pativu ceytuḷḷār. rāparṭ ṭi nopili, vīramāmuṉivar. cīkaṉpālku, ellīs, irēṉiyas, pōp, kālṭuvel, percival, ṭeylar, kiḷārk, irāṭlar, viṉculō, turu ākiya airōppiyarkaḷ tamiḻukkuc ceyta paṇi kuṟitta vivaraṅkaḷi innūlil pativuceytuḷḷār.
kālaṉiyattiṉ mūlam uruvāṉa putiya taṉmikaḷ namatu paṇpāṭṭil palvēṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷik koṇṭuvantaṉa. atil mutaṉmiyāṉatu accup paṇpāṭē eṉṟu collalām. ivvakiyāṉa accup paṇpāṭṭi uruvākkiyavarkaḷ kiṟittuva pātiriyārkaḷ eṉṟāl mikiyilli. ivvaralāṟṟi innūl ciṟappākavē pativu ceytuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
ittokuppukaḷ uruvākkattil toṭakka kālattil utaviya āyvāḷarkaḷ mā. apirāmi, pa. caravaṇaṉ ākiyōrukkum ittokutikaḷ accākum pōtu piḻittiruttam ceytu utaviya āyvāḷarkaḷ vi. tēvēntiraṉ, nā. kaṇṇatācaṉ ākiyōrukkum naṉṟi.
ceṉṉi - 96
ēpral 2010			taṅkaḷ
vī. aracu
tamiḻp pērāciriyar
tamiḻ ilakkiyattuṟi
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam



aṟiñar mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi
“aintaṭikku uṭpaṭṭa kuṟaḷ vaṭivam; paḷapaḷakkum vaḻukkit tali; veṇmi paṭarnta puruvaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭum akaṉṟa neṟṟi; kaṉavu kāṇum kaṇṇimikaḷik koṇṭa vaṭṭa mukam; eṭuppāṉa mūkku; paṭapaṭaveṉap pēcat tuṭikkum mellutaṭukaḷ; kaṇukkāl teriyak kaṭṭiyirukkum nāṉku muḻa veḷḷi vēṭṭi; kālar illāta muḻukkic caṭṭi; caṭṭip piyil mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭi; pavuṇṭaṉ pēṉā; kaḻuttic cuṟṟi mārpiṉ irupuṟamum toṅkum mēl uttarīyam; iṭatu karattil toṅkik koṇṭirukkum puttakap pi. ippaṭiyāṉa tōṟṟattuṭaṉ ceṉṉi miyūciyatti aṭutta kaṉṉimārā lipreriyi viṭṭu vēkamāka naṭantu veḷiyē varukiṟārē! avartāṉ mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ.”
eḻuttāḷar nāraṇa. turikkaṇṇaṉ avarkaḷiṉ mēṟkaṇṭa vivarippu, aṟiñar mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷik kaṇmuṉ kāṇum kāṭci aṉupavattit tarukiṟatu.  tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷāmal, illaṟat tuṟaviyāka vāḻntavar.  eṇpatāṇṭu vāḻkkik kālattil, aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷ muḻumiyākat tamiḻiyal āyvup paṇikku otukkiyavar.  
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil pala putiya taṉmikaḷ naṭimuṟikku vantaṉa. accu entirattip paravalākap payaṉpaṭuttum vāyppu uruvāṉatu. cuvaṭikaḷiliruntu accukkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷ māṟṟap paṭṭaṉa. itaṉmūlam puttaka uruvākkam, itaḻiyal uruvākkam, nūl patippu ākiya pala tuṟikaḷ uruvāyiṉa. ik kālaṅkaḷiltāṉ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ paravalāka aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.  ilakkiya, ilakkaṇap piratikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭatippōl, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tolpaḻaṅkālam kuṟittum pala putiya kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ veḷivantaṉa. pirittāṉiyar kaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi pala putiya varalāṟṟut taravukaḷi veḷikkoṇṭu vantatu. pārampariyac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. tolleḻuttukkaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.  palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷ paṭi eṭukkappaṭṭu vācikkappaṭṭaṉa.  tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ eḻuttumuṟi, ilakkiya, ilakkaṇa uruvākkamuṟi ākiyavi kuṟittu, intak kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ mūlam putitāka aṟiyappaṭṭatu.  akaḻvāyvukaḷ vaḻipeṟappaṭṭa kācukaḷ putiya ceytikaḷi aṟiya  aṭippaṭiyāka amintaṉa. vaṭakku, teṟku eṉa intiyāviṉ paṇpāṭṭup purital cintucamaveḷi akaḻvāyvu mūlam putiya vivātaṅkaḷukku vaḻikaṇṭatu.
tamiḻakac cūḻalil, tolporuḷ āyvukaḷ vaḻi pala putiya kūṟukaḷ uṟutippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.  āticcanallūr, arikkamēṭu akaḻ vāyvukaḷ; tamiḻakattiṉ palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟukku muntiya kālap poruṭkaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ ākiyavi tamiḻaka varalāṟṟip putiya talimuṟiyil eḻutuvataṟku aṭikōliṉa. mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ mēlē vivarikkappaṭṭa cūḻaliltāṉ, tamatu āyvup paṇiyit toṭaṅkiṉār.
vēṅkaṭacāmi cuyamariyāti iyakkac cārpāḷarāka vāḻvit toṭaṅkiṉār.  piṉṉar puttam, camaṇam ākiya camayaṅkaḷ kuṟitta akkaṟi uṭiyavarāka iruntār. ivvaki maṉaniliyōṭu, tamiḻc cūḻalil uruvāṉa putiya nikaḻvukaḷik kuṟittu āyvuceyyat toṭaṅkiṉār.  kiṟittavam, puttam, camaṇam ākiya camayaṅkaḷ, tamiḻiyalukkuc ceyta paṇikaḷip pativu ceytār. ivvakip pativukaḷ tamiḻil putiya tuṟikaḷi aṟimukappaṭuttiṉa.  putiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ mūlam, tamiḻc camūkap paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷi eḻutiṉār.  caṅka ilakkiyap piratikaḷ, pirāmi kalveṭṭukaḷ, piṟa kalveṭṭukaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi varalāṟu eḻutuvataṟkut taravukaḷākak koṇṭār. kalikaḷiṉmītu īṭupāṭu uṭiya maṉa niliyiṉarākavē vēṅkaṭacāmi iḷami mutal iruntār. tamiḻk kali varalāṟṟi eḻutum paṇiyilum tammi īṭupaṭuttik koṇṭār. kaṭṭaṭam, ciṟpam, ōviyam toṭarpāṉa ivaratu āyvukaḷ, tamiḻc camūka varalāṟṟukkup putiya varavāka amintaṉa.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālaṅkaḷil intiyaviyal eṉṟa vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟum pēcappaṭṭatu.  intiya viyalit tirāviṭa iyalākap paṭippaṭiyāka aṭiyāḷap paṭuttum ceyal uruppeṟṟatu.  ip paṇiyil tammi muḻumiyāka īṭupaṭutti yavar vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ.  iṉṟu, tirāviṭa iyal tamiḻiyalāka vaḷarntuḷḷatu.  iv vaḷarccikku vittiṭṭa pala aṟiñarkaḷuḷ vēṅkaṭacāmi mutaṉmiyāṉa paṅkaḷippāḷar āvār. 
mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya - intiya ilakkiyac ciṟpikaḷ mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi eṉṟa nūli cākittiya akātemikkāka eḻutumpōtu ittokuti kaḷi uruvākkiṉēṉ. appōtu avaṟṟi veḷiyiṭa naṇparkaḷ vē. iḷaṅkō, ār. irājārāmaṉ ākiyōr tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar. āṉāl atu naṭipeṟavilli. attokutikaḷ ippōtu veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.
iḷaṅkaṇi patippakam pāvalar pāvēntar pāratitācaṉiṉ aṉittup paṭippukaḷiyum orē vīccil ‘pāvēntam’ eṉum talippil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷati tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.  anta variciyil mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷiṉ uḻippāl viḷinta aṟivut tēṭalkaḷi orē vīccil poruḷvaḻip pirittu muḻumimikka paṭippukaḷāka 1998il  uruvākkiṉēṉ. ataṉi veḷiyiṭa iḷaṅkaṇip patippakam ippōtu muṉvantuḷḷatu.  itaṉip pārāṭṭi makiḻkiṟēṉ.  tamiḻarkaḷ ittokutikaḷi vāṅkip payaṉpeṟuvar eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
- vī. aracu


mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi āyvukaḷ
-	cuyamariyāti iyakka itaḻkaḷil ceytik kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻutuvatit tamatu toṭakka eḻuttup payiṟciyāka ivar koṇṭiruntār. atu ivaruṭiya kaṇṇōṭṭa vaḷarcciyil kuṟippiṭattakka celvākkic celuttiyuḷḷatu.
-	kiṟittava capikaḷiṉ varukiyāl tamiḻil uruvāṉa navīṉa vaḷarccikaḷip pativu ceyyum vakiyil tamatu mutal nūli ivar uruvākkiṉār.  tamiḻ urinaṭi, tamiḻ accu nūl pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷ toṭarpāṉa āvaṇam atuvākum.
-	puttam tamiḻukkuc ceyta paṅkaḷippi matippīṭu ceyyum niliyil ivaratu aṭuttak kaṭṭa āyvu vaḷarntatu.  puttak katikaḷ moḻiyākkam maṟṟum tokuppu, putta jātakak katit tokuppu, kutama puttar vāḻkki varalāṟu eṉṟa pala nilikaḷil puttam toṭarpāṉa āyvup paṅkaḷippi vēṅkaṭacāmi ceytuḷḷār.
-	camaṇa camayam mītu īṭupāṭu uṭiyavarāka vēṅkaṭacāmi iruntār. maṇimēkali, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, ākiyavaṟṟi āyvatiṉ mūlam tamiḻc cūḻalil camaṇa varalāṟṟi āyvu ceytuḷḷār.  camaṇa camaya aṭippaṭikaḷi virivākap pativu ceytuḷḷār.  camaṇac ciṟpaṅkaḷ, kuṟitta ivaratu āyvu taṉit taṉmiyāṉatu.
-	palvēṟu cācaṉaṅkaḷ putitākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.  ōlic cuvaṭikaḷiliruntu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ accu vākaṉam ēṟiṉa. intap piṉpulattil ki.mu. 5 mutal ki.mu. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu muṭiya uḷḷa tamiḻc camūkattiṉ āṭci varalāṟṟi ivar āyvu ceytār.  pallava maṉṉarkaḷ mūvar kuṟitta taṉittaṉi nūlkaḷip paṭittār.  itil tamiḻakac ciṟpam maṟṟum kōyil kaṭṭaṭakkali varalāṟṟiyum āyvu ceytār.
-	aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷic coṟpoḻivi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu,  tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ kāla āyvilum ivar akkaṟi celuttiṉār.  tolkāppiyam, cilappatikāram kuṟitta kāla āyvil ca. viyāpurippiḷḷi pōṉṟōr karutti maṟuttu āyvu nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.  ic coṟpoḻiviṉ iṉṉoru pakutiyāka caṅkak kālac camūkam toṭarpāṉa āyvukaḷilum kavaṉam celuttiṉār.
-	ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷic coṟpoḻivil cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉi āyvup poruḷākkiṉār. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka ki.pi. 3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku  muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟṟip pala nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyuḷḷār. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar, pala kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ kuṟitta virivāṉa āyvi vēṅkaṭacāmi nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.  itaṉ toṭarcciyākak kaḷappirar toṭarpāṉa āyviyum ceytuḷḷār.  iv vāyppukaḷiṉ oru pakutiyāka aṉṟiya tolleḻuttukkaḷ kuṟitta kaḷa āyvu cārntu, āyvukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷār.
-	ovvoru tēciya iṉattiṟkum ataṉ pārampariyac ceḻumi kuṟitta aṟiyum taravukaḷ tēvippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟi āvaṇap paṭuttuvatu mikavum avaciyamākum. maṟintu pōṉavaṟṟit tēṭum muyaṟci atil mukkiyamāṉatākum.  ip paṇiyiyum vēṅkaṭacāmi mēṟkoṇṭirun tār.  ariya taravukaḷi ivar namakku āvaṇappaṭuttit tantuḷḷār.
-	tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kali varalāṟṟi eḻutuvatil vēṅkaṭacāmi akkaṟi celuttiṉār.  pala ariya takavalkaḷi ilakkiyam maṟṟum cācaṉaṅkaḷ vaḻi tokuttuḷḷār.  avaṟṟik kuṟittu cārpu niliyil niṉṟu āyvu ceytuḷḷār.  āyvāḷarukkuriya nērmi, vivēkam, kōpam ākiyavaṟṟi ivvāyvukaḷil kāṇalām.
-	patippu, moḻipeyarppu ākiya paṇikaḷilum vēṅkaṭacāmi īṭupaṭṭati aṟiya muṭikiṟatu.
-	ivaratu āyvup pātiyiṉ cuvaṭukaḷik kāṇumpōtu, tamiḻiyal toṭarpāṉa āvaṇappaṭuttam, tamiḻarukkāṉa varalāṟṟu varivu, tamiḻt tēciya iṉattiṉ kali varalāṟu maṟṟum avikaḷ kuṟitta ivaratu karuttu nili ākiya ceyalpāṭukaḷi nām kāṇalām.

tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu
kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum
kuṟippu: mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ kiṟittavamum tamiḻum (1936) eṉṉum talippil eḻutiya nūl.

niṉivu

eṉakkuk kalviyaṟivip pukaṭṭit tamiḻccuvi ūṭṭiyaruḷiya eṉ arumit tamiyaṉār mayili cīṉi. kōvintarācaṉār avarkaḷiṉ niṉivu malarāka innūl ilaṅkuka.
						     - nūlāciriyar.



muṉṉuri
(mutaṟpatippu) 
tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarccipeṟṟa muḻu varalāṟṟiyum aṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṉum ārvam eṉakku neṭunāḷāka uṇṭu. ākiyāl, camayam vāyttapōtellām attuṟiyil ārāycci ceytuvantēṉ. ivvāṟirukkuṅkāl, 1934 -ām āṇṭiṉ kaṭiciyil, vittuvāṉ tiru. te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār avarkaḷ, M.A., B.L., ceṉṉiyiṟ kūṭṭiya tamiḻ mānāṭṭil, ceṉṉik kiṟittavak kallūrit tamiḻppērāciriyarākiya tiru. ca.ta. caṟkuṇar avarkaḷ, B.A., “kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum” eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi oru coṟpoḻivu nikaḻttiṉārkaḷ. ataṉik kēṭṭapōtu, tamiḻmoḻic carittira ārāycciyil karuttic celuttiniṉṟa eṉakkup putiyatōr ūkkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kiṟittuva camayattavarāl tamiḻmoḻi aṭinta naṉmikaḷik kūṟum tamiḻnūl itukāṟum illāmiyāl, mutalil itaṉi eḻutuvatu tāṉ nalam eṉakkaruti, ittuṟiyil eṉ karuttic celutti ārāycciceyyat toṭaṅkiṉēṉ. itaṉiṭiyil 1935 -ām āṇṭiṉ kaṭiciyil, ilaṅki tirumiku. vipulānanta cuvāmikaḷ, B.Sc., (London), ceṉṉikku vantiruntapoḻutu, avarkaḷiṭam eṉatu ārāycciyit terivittēṉ. cuvāmikaḷ, itu avaciyam eḻutavēṇṭiya pakutitāṉ eṉṟu terivittu, ivvārāycci campantamākat taṅkaḷ karuttukkaḷiyum terivittu eṉṉi ūkkappaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. ituvē ipputtakam eḻutappaṭṭataṉ varalāṟu. innūliluḷḷa kuṟṟaṅkaḷi nīkkik kuṇaṅkaḷiyē koḷḷumpaṭi periyōri vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.
innūlukku mukavuri eḻutiyaruḷumāṟu ilaṅki tirumiku. vipulānanta cuvāmikaḷik kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ. cuvāmikaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ eḻutiyaruḷiṉārkaḷ.  itaṟkiṭiyil putitoru yōcaṉi tōṉṟiyatu. tamiḻ ilaṅkikkum tamiḻ intiyāvukkum tamiḻt talimi pūṇṭu viḷaṅkum tirumiku. cuvāmikaḷ aruḷiya mukavuri yoṉṟē cālum eṉṟālum, innūl intu ulakattukku maṭṭumaṉṟi, kiṟittuva ulakattukkum uriyatākaliṉ, kiṟittuvat tamiḻaṟiñariṉ mukavuriyum innūlukku avaciyam eṉat tōṉṟiyatu. kiṟittuva matattil kattōlik, puroṭṭasṭeṇṭu eṉṉu irupirivukaḷ iruppatāl, avviru pirivukaḷiṉ cārpāka iraṇṭu tamiḻp periyōrkaḷi mukavuri eḻutiyutavumāṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ. eṉatu vēṇṭukōḷuk kiṇaṅki, mēl nāṭṭu moḻikaḷ kīḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟil pulami vāyntu, tamiḻc coṟpiṟappārāycci ceytuvanta periyār, yāḻppāṇattu nallūr uyartiru. cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar avarkaḷ, O.M.I., oru nūṉmukam eḻuti aruḷiṉārkaḷ. avvāṟē, eṉatu vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅki, taṅkaḷ uttiyōka kālam pōka mikutiyuḷḷa kālamellām, taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil, yātoru ūtiyamum karutāmal māṇavarkaḷukkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷip pāṭañcolli, avarkaḷic ciṟanta tamiḻppulavarāka viḷaṅkac ceytuvarukiṟa naṟkuṇap periyār, ceṉṉik kiṟittuvak kallūrit tamiḻp pērāciriyar tiru. ca.ta. caṟkuṇar avarkaḷ, B.A., āṅkila mukavuri eḻutiyaruḷiṉārkaḷ. ākavē, innūl mūṉṟu mukavurikaḷuṭaṉ vecaḷivarukiṟatu. inta mukavurikaḷi eḻutiyaruḷiya mūṉṟu periyārkaḷukkum eṉatu maṉamārnta vantaṉam uriyatākum.
innūliṟ cērppataṟkāka, tiru. va. cuppiyā piḷḷiyavarkaḷ, ṭākṭar ji.yū. pōp aiyar, ṭākṭar kālṭvel aiyar, paṭaṅkaḷik koṭuttutaviyataṟkāka avarkaḷukkum eṉatu vantaṉam uriyatākum.
mayilāppūr,
ceṉṉi, 24-11-36.	mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi

muṉṉuri
(iraṇṭām patippu) 
ic ciṟu nūlip peritum matittu, nalluri eḻutiya periyār palarkkum, naṉkoṭi utaviya ceṉṉip pāṭacālip puttaka ilakkiya caṅkattārkkum, 1940 ām āṇṭiṉ iṇṭermīṭiyeṭ parīṭcikkup pāṭamāka vitittaruḷiya palkalikkaḻakap periyōrkkum peritum vantaṉam kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
mayilāppūr,
ceṉṉi, 10-7-38.	mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi

muṉṉuri
(mūṉṟām patippu) 
iraṇṭām patippu veḷivantu pattu āṇṭukaḷāy viṭṭaṉa. ippōtu innūl mūṉṟām patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. muṟpatippu kaḷil, āṅkāṅku irunta cila coṟkaḷi nīkki avaṟṟiṟkup patilākat tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷic cērntiruppatum, iraṇṭoru iṭaṅkaḷil putitākac cila ceytikaḷic cērttiruppatum, tavira, atikamāka vēṟu karuttukkaḷi itil cērkkavilli. tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattār, ippatippic cemmipeṟa nalla muṟiyil acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiyataṟkāka avarkaḷukku eṉatu vantaṉam uriyatākum.
mayilāppūr,
ceṉṉi, navampar, 48.	mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi

muṉṉuri
(nāṉkāmpatippu) 
kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum eṉṉum innūl ippōtu nāṉkām patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. ippatippil persival aiyar, teylar aiyar, pavurī ciyar, kiḷārk aiyar, rāṭlar aiyar, uviṉslō aiyar, turu aiyar, pavar aiyar eṉpavarkaḷip paṟṟiya ciṟukuṟippum, ekara, okara eḻuttukkaḷip paṟṟiya piṉṉiṇippum putitākac cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattār, innāṉkām patippi nalla muṟiyil acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiyataṟkāka avarkaḷukku eṉatu vantaṉattit terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.
mayilāppūr,			
ceṉṉi-4,		      mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi
20-5-55.

mukavuri I
innūlāciriyar āṇṭil iḷiyarāyiṉum ārāyccit tuṟiyil mutirntavar. teṉṉintiyāvilum īḻa nāṭṭilum aṇmiyil vāḻnta tamiḻaṟiñar palaruṭiya vāḻkki varalāṟu, aṉṉā riyaṟṟiya nūl varalāṟu eṉṉum ivitammit teḷivupeṟa ārāyntaṟiyavēṇṭumeṉṉum peruviruppa muṭiyavar. pala āṇṭukaḷāka nāṉ ivari naṉkaṟivēṉ; nalloḻukkam vāyntavar; nallōruṭiya kūṭṭuṟavip poṉṉēpōṟ pōṟṟupavar.
innūliṉakattē ivar tokuttuvittirukkiṉṟa muṭipukaḷ ivarāṟ pala āṇṭukaḷāka ārāyntu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. palappala āṅkila nūlkaḷilum, tamiḻnūlkaḷilum, parantukiṭanta uṇmikaḷi oruṅku tiraṭṭic cevvi peṟa vakuttu, nūluruvamākki veḷiyiṭ ṭirukkiṉṟār. innūl kallūrimāṇavarkkum, tamiḻaṟiñarkkum, teṉṉintiya carittira ārāycciyāḷarkkum iṉṟiyamiyāta toṉṟākum. ātaliṉ, itaṉit tamiḻulakam uvantēṟṟu ātarikku meṉpataṟ kiyamilli. in nūlāciriyar iṉṉum pala ārāyccikaḷ ceytu, tamiḻakattiṟkup payaṉpaṭa vāḻumāṟu ellām valla iṟivaṉ iṉṉaruḷ purivāṉāka.
civāṉanta vittiyālayam,
kallaṭiyuppōṭi,			cuvāmi vipulāṉantar.
kāttāṉkuṭi, cilōṉ.		 		19-11-36.

mukavuri II
orukālam mikac cīrtiruttam aṭintu muḻuulakattiṉ cīrtiruttattukkum mēlvaric caṭṭamāyt tikaḻntirunta intiyāviṉ makārākiya nām, iṭikkālattil ac cīrtiruttatti orupuṭi iḻantupōyiṉōm. emakku vekukālattiṉ piṉṉar nākarikam peṟṟōrākiya airōppiyarē, nām pala tuṟikaḷiṉ maḻuṅka viṭṭirunta nākarikatti mīṇṭum aṭiya, emakkuk kaṭavuḷ kaṭāṭcattāl tuṇiceyvōrāṉārkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻ vaḷarccikku mikka upakāramāṉa acciyantira upāyatti em nāṭṭil varuvittatu aṉṉōr ceyta naṉṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟām. tamiḻil taṉi vāṉa nūlkaḷi ākkum iyakkattit toṭakkiviṭṭavarum, neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyāyc coṟkaḷiṉ arttatti viḷakkum vaṉappu vāynta tamiḻ akarātiyic ceytu tantavarum airōppiya kurumārē. viññāṉattuṟikaḷiṟ cila nūlkaḷi mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil iyaṟṟiṉōrum, tamiḻariṉ kalviyi viruttiyākkum poruṭṭu āṅkila pāṭacālikaḷit tāpittōrum, avaṟṟiṉ mūlamāyp pala kalikaḷukku uriya nūlkaḷit tamiḻil iyaṟṟiyum iyaṟṟuvittum tamiḻ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup pērutavi ceytōrum airōppiyak kiṟittuva camaya kuravarē. muṉṉāḷil camaṇap periyōr tamiḻiṟkut tirukkuṟaḷ, cīvakacintāmaṇi ākiya centamiḻ nūlkaḷi utaviyatupōla, piṉṉāḷil tēmpāvaṇi ākiya pattiya nūlkaḷi utaviyavarum ōr airōppiya kuravarē. it tamiḻvallunarkaḷip piṉ toṭarntu nam nāṭṭuk kiṟittuvarkaḷum, avvak kālattē tamiḻiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukku uriya pala toṇṭukaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar.
ivvāṟu, airōppiyarum tamiḻnāṭṭavarumāṉa kiṟittavar kaḷāl tamiḻum tamiḻulakum peṟṟukkoṇṭa pērupakāraṅkaḷic curukkamāyk kūṟuvatāy, iḷiñarum kirakittukkoḷḷattakka peṟṟiyāṉa tamiḻnaṭi poruntiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟa “kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum” eṉap peyar cūṭiya nūlip pārttu āṉanta vacattarāṉōm. “tiṉittuṇi naṉṟi ceyiṉum paṉit tuṇiyāk koḷvar payaṉ terivār” eṉṟapaṭi, nammaṉōrellām kiṟittavar tamiḻukkuc ceyta naṉṟiyi aṟintu pārāṭṭumpaṭiyāka icciṟiya nūlic ciṟappuṟa eḻutittanta srī mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmikkum, aḻakuṟap patippitta srī. kā. ē. vaḷḷi nātaṉukkum tamiḻulakam mikak kaṭappāṭu uṭiyatākiṟatu.
nallūr,
yāḻppāṇam.		        		  cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar.

Foreword III
The most brilliant age in Tamil literature, the so-called Sangam Age, came to a close at the downfall of the three Tamil Dynasties, and was succeeded by dark ages which continued all through the long period of anarchy and misrule in Southern India. It was after the advent of Europeans in general, and European Christian Missionaries in particular, that the revival of Tamil letters began.
When Europeans realised that the best way of knowing and influencing the Tamil people, among whom they came to live and work, was through the knowledge of their language, they applied themselves closely to the study of Tamil. They found it was a noble language with a noble literature, but was generally neglected by its people, minly by reason of its literary dialect being almost entirely poetical, and so different from the spoken dialect, that one, who could read and write the language as spoken, could not even divide a line of poetry into its component words, unless one had made a special study of it for years.
So they seem to have thought it their first duty to try to open the eyes of the Tamils to the greatness of their language. With this object in view, many of them have expressed their opinion of Tamil, its literature, and grammar. Their opinions are very useful and interesting, but we have space here only for a few of them.
First, as regards the language : Dr. Winslow writes, “It is not perhaps extravagant to say that in its poetic from the Tamil is more polished and exact than the Greek, and, in both dialects, with its borrowed treasures, more copious than the Latin. In its fulness and power, it more resembles English and German than any other living language.” Dr. Schimid writes of Tamil that “the mode of collocating its words follows the logical or intellectual order more so than even the Latin or Greek.” The Rev. P. Percival observes, “Perhaps no language combines greater force with equal brevity; and it may be asserted that no human speech is more close and philisophic in its expression as an exponent of the mind. The sequence of things - of thought, action, and its results - is always mintined inviolate. “Professor Whitney writes that he “was informed by an American who was born in South India and grew up to speak its language vernacularly along with his English, a man of high education and unusual gifts as a preacher and writer, that he esteemed the Tamil a finer language to think and speak in than any European tongue known to him.”
Dr. G. Slater, late professor of Indian Economics in the 
University of Madras, writes that “the Tamil language is extrordinary in its subtlety and sense of logic.” and that “the perfcection with which it has been developed into an organ for precise and subtle thought, combined with the fact that it represents a much earlier stage in the evolution of infexional language than any Indo germanic tongue, suggests................the priority of the Dravidians in attining settled order and regular Government,” and that “as it is known to us it the product of a very long period of a somewhat elaborate civilisation.”
Secondly, as regards its literature: That “unique genius,” Father Beschi, writes that “the Tamil poets use the genuine language of poetry.”and “there are excellent works in Tamil poetry on the subject of the divine attributes and the nature of virtue.” Dr. Pope, referring to the numerous ethic works in Tamil, remarks, “I have felt sometimes as if there must be a blessing in store for a people that delight so utterly in compositions thus remarkably expressive of a hunger and thirst after righteousness.” The Rev. H. Bower, a Eurasian, writes of the Tamil Poetess Auviyar, that “She sang like Sappho, yet not of love, but of virtue,” and of the Chintamani that “it is a moral epic of the highest merit,” and of the Sacred Kural that the “work is superior to the institutes of Manu and is worthy of the divine Plato himself.”
Thirdly, as regards its grammar: Rev. T. Brotherton wirtes that “it is generally allowed by all who are at all conversant with Tamil literature, that the Nunnool or Tamil Grammar of Pavananti, is the most philosophically and logically arranged Grammar of any 
language in the universe, and that it is a “venerable and esteemed work.”
Indian Christians have sid none the less. One of them writes of the Sacred Kural and its author that “it is refreshing to think that a nation which has produced so great a man and so unique a work cannot be a hopeless, desciple race.” Another often observed that “one of the min objects of studying Tamil literature is to know and catch the genius of the ancient Tamil people” And another wrote : “Indeed, the various kinds of elliptical constructions in which Tamil greatly delights, the philosophic sequence of things that it always mintins inviolate, the logical and musical choice of terms that it often makes from a veritable host of synonyms, the implied significations that it often presents to the careful reader, the numerous and extrordinary poetic licenses that it enjoys, and, above all, the extreme elasticity by which it possesses a large amount of linguistic economy and is capable of being interpreted according to the intellectual capacity of the reader, these - these alone, which none can deny it, are likely to make any one, who is rightly and faithfully devoted to its literature, a person of no mean mental power..... There is no easier and better method of guging the genius and mental acumen of the ancient Tamil race than to study their science of grammar which they have carried to a very high degree of prefection. Theie subtle lingusitic observation, their happy invention of the wonderful Sutra-device, their logical arrangment of the rules, and their elaborate methods of construing them cannot but be the wonder of all ages.”
After many such eulogies, European and Indian Christians have exhorted the Tamils not to neglect their language. They have also pointed out that neglect of vernacular means only national suicide. Dr. Pope wrote, “Let the Tamils cease to be ashamed of their Tamil.” Dr. Murdoch wrote to them, “Let them be assured that so long as they despise their mother tongue they have merely a thin whitewash of civilisation,” and quoted the words of Von F. Schlagel. “That acquaintance with foreign languages, whether dead or living, which is necessary for men of letters and fashion, is no longer associated with neglect of their vernacular speech, a neglact which is always sure to work its own revenge on those who practise it, and which can never be supposed to create any prejudice either in favour of their politeness or their erudition.”
Now, even if Europeans have not done anything else than trying thus to open our eyes to the excellence of our langugage, and warning us aginst neglecting it, we must be extremely indebted to them. But they have done more.
They simplified the Tamil script. They introduced space between the words, simple or compound, of a sentence, for it had been the native custom to write a whole sentence as if it were composed of one long word. They were the first to print Tamil tracts, books, and papers. They were the first to introduce the study of Tamil inot the civilised countries of the world. They were the first to make Tamil translations from European languages. They were the first to compile Tamil word-books, dictionaries and lexicons, which made obsolete the time-honoured custom of memorising the metrical Tamil vocabularies, before taking up literature. They were the first to write Tamil treatises on Natural Science. They were the first to urge educated Tamils to bring out expurgated editions of Tamil works, and to write interesting general literature of a wholesome character. They were the first to show us how to make a critical study of our language, literature, and grammar. And they were the first not only to teach us that “that is not good language that all understand not,” and that there can be no social, moral, religious, political, or economical progress among a people who have no homely prose literature to read, but also to write “Modern Tamil prose,” and create the reading habit among us.
The above lines contin a brief summary of a long public Tamil lecture, “Christianity and Tamil,” delivered by me about two years ago, in Madras. The author of this book, who is a broad-minded Hindu School-Master, says that the lecture roused his curiosity, and gave him the incentive to make a study of the subject himself and the book is a result of it.
The book contins a very important chapter, which is not 
generally known, in the history of Tamil Language and Literature. The short biographical sketches and notes of some of the European and native Christian Tamil Scholars, and the well-selected 
specimens of Tamil writing of some of the European scholars 
cannot fail to be quite interesting. We have here fine specimens of the flowery language of the Italian Tamil Scholar, Father De Nobilibus; of the simple, direct, and telling language of the Swiss, the Rev. C.T. E. Rhenius, on the Truthfulness of the Gospels; of the poetical and literary, and colloquial prose style of the Italian, Father C.J. Beschi, the greatest of the European Tamil Scholars; who 
expresses, in one of the passage quoted here by the author, his high appreciation of the Sacred Kural.
The book is well written too. Its language is simple, correct, natural, and free from all tricks of style.
The book is sure to make all Tamil students feel extremely proud of the literature and grammar of their language, and love and study and improve it with zeal.
The author has my great thanks, and the book, my best wishes.

Madras,		S.D. SARGUNAR
1-11-1986	Leturer in Tamil
Madras Christian College.

1. kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum
1. airōppiyar varuki
kiṟittuvarākiya airōppiyariṉ toṭarpiṉāl tamiḻ moḻikku uṇṭāṉa vaḷarcciyi ārāyvatē innūliṉ nōkkam. ataṉi ārāyap pukuvataṟku muṉ airōppiyariṉ toṭarpu eppaṭi ēṟpaṭṭateṉpatit terivippatu muṟiyākum. ākiyāl, airōppiyar namatu tēcattiṟku vanta varalāṟṟic curukkamāka eḻutuvōm.
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē, mēlnāṭṭār namatu intiyā tēcattuṭaṉ, ciṟappākat teṉ intiyāvuṭaṉ, vaṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākat terikiṟatu. yavaṉar1 eṉṉum airōppiyak kulattār cēra nāṭṭiṉ kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṟ paṇṭakacālikaḷ amittu, avaṟṟil namatu nāṭṭuc carakkukaḷic cēmittuvittuk kappalkaḷ mūlamāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ. maturi, pukār2 mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷilum yavaṉar vacittu vantatākat tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉāl aṟikiṟōm. ki.mu. 22-il, maturiyil aracāṇṭirunta pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ, akastascīcar3 eṉṉum yavaṉa aracaṉiṭam tūtuvarkaḷi aṉuppiṉāṉ eṉṟu strēpo4 eṉṉum mēl nāṭṭāciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṟār. tamiḻarukkum yavaṉarukkum irunta iv vaṇikat toṭarpu ki.pi. 47 mutal mēṉmēlum atikappaṭṭatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, anta āṇṭil hippalas5 eṉpavar, intu mākaṭalil vīcukiṟa vaṭakiḻakku, teṉmēṟkup paruvakkāṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittār. ip paruvakkāṟṟu vīcukiṟa kālaṅkaḷiṟ pirayāṇam ceyvatāl, mālumikaḷ virivākak kappalkaḷic celuttik kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟakkuṟiyak kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟ cellakkūṭiyatāyiruntatu. ākavē, yavaṉa vaṇikarkaḷ atikamākat tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku varat talippaṭṭārkaḷ. iv vaṇikap perukkattiṉāl namatu tēcattārukku ērāḷamāṉa varuvāy kiṭittatu. “intiyā tēcam ērāḷamāṉa celvatti yavaṉa tēcattiliruntu āṇṭutōṟum kavarntu koḷkiṟatu.” eṉṟu piḷiṉi eṉṉum āciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṟār. intat tamiḻar - yavaṉar vaṇikat toṭarpippaṟṟip puṟanāṉūṟu, maṇimēkali, cilappatikāram mutaliya caṅkanūlkaḷilum kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻarukkum yavaṉarukkum irunta intat toṭarpu vāṇikat tōṭumaṭṭum niṉṟiruntatē tavira camayam, kali, moḻi mutaliyavikaḷiṟ ciṟitum iṭampeṟavilli. yavaṉar nammuṭaṉ nērmukamākak koṇṭirunta inta vaṇikattoṭarpu ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvariyil nilipeṟṟiruntatu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, arapi tēcattu mukamatiyarkaḷ ekiptu, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷi veṉṟu kippaṟṟiya piṟaku, yavaṉar namatu tēcattuṭaṉ vittirunta nērmukamāṉa vaṇikattoṭarpu taṭippaṭṭu viṭṭatu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟaku mukamatiyar kaṭalvaḻiyiyum tarivaḻiyiyum kippaṟṟi ātikkam peṟṟatōṭu, intiya vāṇikattiyum kippaṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. intiyap poruḷkaḷi vāṅkik koṇṭupōy airōppiya tēcaṅkaḷil viṟpaṉi ceyvatāṟ perumporuḷ ūtiyaṅ kiṭippatik kaṇṭu, mukamatiyar airōppiyari intiyāvuṭaṉ nērmukamāka vāṇikam ceyya voṭṭātapaṭi taṭuttuviṭṭārkaḷ. ivvitamākap paṇṭikkālattil airōppiyar namatu tēcattuṭaṉ koṇṭirunta vaṇikat toṭarpu aṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. mukammatiyar maliyāḷak kariyiṟ paṇṭakacālikaḷi amittu, miḷaku mutaliya poruḷkaḷic coṟpa vilikku ērāḷamāy vāṅkic cēmittuvittu, avaṟṟik kappalkaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu arapikkaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṅkaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu, aṅkiruntu tari vaḻiyāka cūyas, keyrō, alaksāntiriyā mutalāṉa nakaraṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭupōy, oṉṟukkup paṉmaṭaṅku atikamāka vilivittuc carakkukaḷi viṟṟu, perumporuḷ tiraṭṭiṉārkaḷ. vaṇikattoḻil ceyyum kulattār eppoḻutum celvantarā yiruppatu ellōrum aṟinta uṇmi. itaṉpaṭi, viyāpārat toḻilil īṭupaṭṭa mukammatiyar mēṉmēlum celvam peṟṟuc ciṟappaṭintu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. airōppiyar ippōtu kittoḻil, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟil ciṟappaṭintiruppatu pōla akkālattiṟ ciṟappup peṟāmal tāḻnta niliyil iruntārkaḷ. ākiyāl, āṭi, mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷ intiyā mutaliya kīḻit tēcaṅkaḷiliruntē airōppāvukku ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭṭuvantaṉa. itaṉāl inta vāṇikattit taṅkaḷ kiyil vittirunta mukammatiyar peruñ celvam campātittatil viyappoṉṟumilli. ivvāṟu cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa.
intiyac carakkukaḷi airōppiya nāṭukaḷiṟ koṇṭu pōy viṟpataṉāl mukammatiyar celvam peṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻvatik kaṇṭa airōppiya tēcattār, tāṅkaḷum nērmukamāka intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikañ ceytu poruḷ īṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu āval koṇṭārkaḷ. āṉāl, intiyāvukkuc cellumvaḻi mukammatiyar ātikkattiliruntapaṭiyālum, vēṟu kulattār ivvaṇikattik kikkoḷḷātapaṭi avarkaḷ (mukammatiyar) veku karuttākak kāval ceytiruntapaṭiyālum, airōppiyariṉ eṇṇam kikūṭavilli. akkālattil pūkōḷa nūl teriyāta paṭiyāl vēṟu vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkuc cellum vaḻiyiyum airōppiyar aṟintirukka villi. intiyā airōppāvukkuk kiḻakkup pakkattil irukkiṉṟa teṉpatumaṭṭum avarkaḷukkut teriyum. āṉāl, aṅkuc celvataṟku vēṟu vaḻi teriyāmal tikittaṉar. akkālattil, airōppāk kaṇṭattil intiyāvippaṟṟip pala katikaḷ vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. intiyā tēcam pūlōka cuvarkkam eṉṟum, paṇam kāykkum maraṅkaḷ aṅkē ērāḷamāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, tēṉ āṟum, pāl āṟum kari puraṇṭōṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum palavitamāṉa ceytikaḷ ulavivantaṉa. ivvitac ceytikaḷik kēṭṭapōtu airōppiyarukku nāvil nīr curantatu. tēṉāṟum pālāṟum kari puraṇṭōṭum antap pūlōka cuvarkkatti, paṇam kāykkum maraṅkaḷ vaḷarukiṟa viyappāṉa intiyā tēcattik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟum, attēcattuṭaṉ nērmukamāka vaṇikat toṭarpu peṟṟup poruḷ campātikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum airōppiyar āvalōṭu virumpiṉārkaḷ. akkālattil airōppā muḻutum intiyāviṉ pēccākavē iruntatu. āṉāl, intiyāvukku eppaṭip pōvatu? intiyāvukkuc cellum vaḻiyi mukammatiyar kippaṟṟit taṅkaḷ ātikkattil vittukkoṇṭu, vēṟu kulattār avvaḻiyākac cellātapaṭi, kaṇṇi imi kāppatupōl kāttu varukiyil, airōppiyar intu tēcam cella niṉittatu, muṭavaṉ tēṉukku ācippaṭṭatupōl iruntatu.
ivvāṟiruntu varukiyil, pūkōḷa nūlippaṟṟi ārāyntu vanta aṟivāḷikaḷ pūmi appaḷampōl taṭṭiyāka irukka villi eṉṟum, pantupōl uruṇṭi vaṭivāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟum aṟintārkaḷ. āṉāl, ippūkōḷanūl uṇmiyi makkaḷ oppukkoḷvataṟku neṭunāḷ ceṉṟatu. kaṭiciyāka, pōrccukal tēcattavarākiya kolampas eṉpavar, ip pūkōḷa nūl uṇmiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, intiyā tēcattiṟkuc cella vēṟoru vaḻiyik kaṇṭupiṭikkat tuṇintār. eppaṭi eṉṟāl, pūmi uruṇṭiyāyiruppatāl, oruvaṉ ōriṭattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mēṟku mukamākap pirayāṇam ceytukoṇṭē pōṉāl, kaṭiciyil avaṉ kiḻakkup pakkamākat tāṉ puṟappaṭṭa iṭattiṟku vantu cērvāṉ eṉṉum pūkōḷa nūl uṇmiyi maṉattiṟkoṇṭu, intiyā tēcam airōppāvukkuk kiḻakkil irukkiṟapaṭiyāl, airōppā viliruntu mēṟkup pakkamāyp pirayāṇam ceytu koṇṭu pōṉāl, intiyā pōyc cēralām eṉṟu kolampas eṉpavar nampiṉār. āṉāl, avar colliya karutti oruvarum ātarikkavilli. kaṭiciyāka, mikunta cipāriciṉ mēl, speyṉ tēcattu aracaṉ kolampasukku utavi ceyya icintu, cila kappalkaḷiyum, mālumikaḷiyum pirayāṇattukku vēṇṭiya cāmāṉkaḷiyum koṭuttutaviṉāṉ. kolampas speyṉ tēcattiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭu, mēṟkup pakkamāyk kaṭaliṟ pirayāṇam ceytu koṇṭu pōṉār. cila nāṭkaḷ pirayāṇam ceyta piṟaku, kolampas mēṟkintiya tīvukaḷiyum, putiya ulakam eṉṉum amerikkāk kaṇṭattiyum kaṇṭupiṭittār. āṉāl, kolampas at tīvukaḷ intiyā tēcattic cērntavi eṉṟum, ak kaṇṭam intiyā tēcam eṉṟum tavaṟāka niṉittār. avar tām kaṇṭupiṭitta pūpākam intiyā tēcamtāṉ eṉkiṟa nampikkiyuṭaṉ iṟantār. āṉāl, uṇmiyil atu intiyā tēcam aṉṟu. “oṉṟi niṉikkiṉ atu oḻintiṭṭoṉṟākum” eṉṟapaṭi, kolampas intiyā tēcattik kaṇṭupiṭikkum nōkkattōṭu cella, taṟceyalākap putiya pūpākaṅkaḷik kāṇap peṟṟār. kolampas putiya ulakattik kaṇṭu piṭitta ceyti airōppiyarkaḷukkup putiya uṇarcciyi aḷittatu. ēṉiya airōppiya cātiyārum intiyā tēcattukkup putiya vaḻiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka ūkkaṅkoṇṭu muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ūkkaṅkoṇṭavarkaḷil pōrccukal tēcattaracaṉum oruvaṉ. emāṉuyeci6 eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa anta aracaṉ āppirikkā kaṇṭattic cuṟṟik koṇṭu kiḻakku mukamākap pirayāṇam ceytāl, intiyā tēcam pōyc cēralām eṉṟu nampikki koṇṭavaṉ. ākiyāl, avvaḻiyākac ceṉṟu intiyā tēcattik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpaṭi vāskō-ṭā-kāmā eṉpavari aṉuppiṉāṉ. vāskō-ṭā-kāmā pōrccukkal tēcattut tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamākiya lispaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭiṉatti viṭṭu 1497 ām varucam cūli mātam 9ām nāḷ puṟappaṭṭuk kaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṉṟapōtu, etirkāṟṟil akappaṭṭu aṉēka iṭar iḻippukaḷuk kuṭpaṭṭār. āṉālum, maṉan taḷarāmal mēṉmēlum kappali yōṭṭik koṇṭu, āppirikkāk kaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyākiya naṉṉampikki muṉiyic cuṟṟik kaṭantu, ak kaṇṭattiṉ kiḻakkuk kariyōramākap pirayāṇam ceytu, kaṭiciyil milāṇṭā7 eṉṉum kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉatti aṭintār. atu ak kālattil pēr peṟṟa tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam. ākiyāl, intiyāviliruntu pala kappalkaḷ aṅku vantiruntaṉa. vāskō-ṭā-kāmā aṅkirunta oru mukammatiya mālumiyi intiyā tēcattukku vaḻi kāṭṭumpaṭi tammuṭaṉ aḻittuk koṇṭu aṅkiruntu puṟappaṭṭu, anta mukammatiya mālumiyiṉ utaviyāl, ki.pi. 1498-ām varucam mē mātam 22ām nāḷ maliyāḷakkariyil uḷḷa kaḷḷik kōṭṭi8 eṉṉum paṭṭiṉatti aṭintār. yavaṉarukkup piṟaku vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭu intiyāvukku vanta kiṟittava matattic cērnta airōppiyar ivar tām. piṟaku, vāskō-ṭā-kāmā kaḷḷikkōṭṭiyi aracāṇṭirunta cāmuttiri aracaṉik9 kaṇṭu pēci, pōrccukal tēcattār kaḷḷikkōṭṭiyil varttakam ceyya avvaracaṉiṭam uttaravu peṟṟuk koṇṭu, mīṇṭum tam ūrukkuc ceṉṟu, tām intiyāvik kaṇṭupiṭitta makiḻvuc ceytiyit tammuṭiya aracaṉukkut terivittār. pōrccukal tēcattaracaṉ mikavum makiḻcci aṭintu, intiyā tēcattuṭaṉ varttakam ceyvataṟkākavum, kūṭumāṉāl, at tēcatti taṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭuttuvataṟkākavum cila kappalkaḷi āyattam ceytu, pīṭrō ālvāres kāprāl10 eṉpavar talimiyiṟ pala pōrccukīciyari aṉuppiṉāṉ. intak kāprāl eṉṉum talivar kappalic celuttikkoṇṭu pōkiṟapōtu. āppirikkāk kaṇṭattiṉ kariyōramākac ceṉṟāl, etirkāṟṟil akappaṭṭu varuntavēṇṭiyirukkum eṉṟu niṉittu, akkaṇṭat tukku mēṟkē atika tūramāyk kaṭaliṟ pōykkoṇṭirukkiyil, teṉ amerikkāvil uḷḷa, ippōtu pirēcil11 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟa ceḻippāṉa tēcattil taṟceyalākap pōyc cērntār. tām pōkak karuti vanta intiyā tēcam cellāmal taṟceyalāy vēṟu oru putiya tēcattiṟ ceṉṟati aṟintu viyappuṟṟu, avar mīṇṭum aṅkiruntu puṟappaṭṭu intiyā tēcam vantu cērntār. pirēcil tēcattiliruntu intiyāvukkup puṟappaṭumpōtu aṅkiruntu cila paḻac ceṭikaḷik koṇṭu vantu intiyāviṟ paravac ceytār. avar koṇṭu vantavi miḷakāy, muntirikkoṭṭi, koyyāppaḻam, aṉācip paḻam, cīttāppaḻam, pappāḷippaḻam mutaliyavi. ippaḻacceṭikaḷ ivar koṇṭu varuvataṟku muṉpu namatu nāṭṭililli.
patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kaṭiciyil namatu nāṭṭiṟku vanta paraṅkiyar (pōrccukīciyarukkup “paṟaṅkiyar” eṉṟu peyar) kocci, kaḷḷikkōṭṭi, kōvā mutaliya kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil amarntu vāṇikam ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷukku muṉ taṉi urimiyāy vāṇikam ceytirunta mukammatiyarukkup paṟaṅkiyar vantu vāṇikam ceyvatu piṭikka villi. itaṉāl paṟaṅkiyarukkum mukammatiyarukkum cila caṇṭikaḷ naṭantaṉa. accaṇṭikaḷiṟ paṟaṅkiyar veṟṟi peṟṟu, mukammatiyari aṭakki, arapikkaṭalit taṅkaḷ āṭciyiṟ koṇṭuvantārkaḷ. atumutal cumār oru nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil paṟaṅkiyarkaḷ intiyā, ilaṅki mutaliya kīḻit tēcaṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceytuvantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ vāṇikam ceyvatōṭu maṭṭum nillāmal, kiṟittuva matattip paravac ceyyavum pāṭupaṭṭārkaḷ. ēcuviṉ capip pātirimārkaḷik koṇṭu vantu, peruntokiyāṉa intukkaḷik kiṟittavarākkiṉārkaḷ. cavēriyār, tattuvapōtaka cuvāmi, jāṉ-ṭi-piriṭ ṭō, vīramā muṉivar mutaliya pātirimār ivarkaḷ aḻittuvanta ēcuviṉ capiyic cērnta tuṟavikaḷāvar. ituvumaṉṟi, paṟaṅkiyar taṅkaḷ vāṇipap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷik kāvalpuriyum poruṭṭu, paṟaṅkip pōr vīrarkaḷiyum koṇṭuvantiruntārkaḷ. ippōrvīrarkaḷ intiyap peṇkaḷi maṇañceytukoḷḷumpaṭi kaṭṭāyap paṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. ivvitak kalappumaṇattiṉāṟ piṟanta piḷḷikaḷ “tuppāci” eṉṉum peyaruṭiya putiya kulattārāṉārkaḷ. intat tuppācikaḷ paṟaṅkimoḻiyip pēcivantaṉar.
ivvitamākap paṟaṅkiyar intiyāvukkup putu vaḻi kaṇṭu piṭittu, intiya tēcattuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytu poruḷ tiraṭṭic celvantarāvatik kaṇṭu ēṉiya airōppiya iṉattārum intiyā tēcattuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceyya āval koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ hālaṇṭu12 eṉṉum nāṭṭiluḷḷa ṭaccukkārarum im muyaṟciyil muṉintu niṉṟārkaḷ. inta ṭaccukkārarukku “ollāntar” eṉpatum peyar. inta ollāntar intiyāvukku vaṭakiḻakku vaḻiyoṉṟip putitākak kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. kolampas airōppāvukku mēṟku mukamāy intiyāvukkup pōka oru vaḻiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṉṟārallavā? paṟaṅkiyar airōppāvukkut teṟku mukamāy āppirikkāvic cuṟṟi kiḻakku mukamākac ceṉṟu, intiyāvukku oru vaḻiyik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷē. atippōlavē tāṅkaḷum airōppāvukku vaṭakiḻakkup pakkamāyc ceṉṟu intiyāvukkup putuvaḻi oṉṟik kaṇṭupiṭikkalāmē eṉṟu ollāntar niṉittārkaḷ. appaṭi niṉittatu akkālattil pūkōḷa cāttiratti avarkaḷ carivara aṟiyavilli eṉpatit terivikkiṟatu. inta eṇṇattōṭu avarkaḷ nāṉku kappalkaḷi intiyāvukku vaṭakiḻakkup pātiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka aṉuppi ṉārkaḷ. inta muyaṟciyil avarkaḷ tōlviyaṭintārkaḷ. āṉāl, muṉpu aṟintirāta cila tīvukaḷiyum, kaṭalkaḷiyum kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ. ollāntar vaṭakiḻakkup pakkamāyp putuvaḻi kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṟci ceyta atēkālattil, vēṟu nāṉku kappalkaḷip paṟaṅkiyar kaṇṭupiṭitta vaḻiyip pārttuvarumpaṭi aṉuppiṉārkaḷ. ikkappalkaḷ kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷil oṉṟākiya cāvā (cāvakam) tīvuvariyiṟceṉṟu tirumpi vantaṉa. atumutal ollāntar intiyā tēcattuṭaṉum kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷuṭaṉum vāṇipam ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ intiya viyāpāratti mutalil taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil naṭatti vantaṉar. piṉṉar 1902 ām varucam mārccu mātam ‘2’ ām nāḷ “kiḻakkintiya varttakac caṅkam” eṉṉum pērāl oru caṅkatti ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭārkaḷ. inta ollāntar patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil paṟaṅkiyari intiyā, ilaṅki mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷiliruntu turattiviṭṭu, avarkaḷirunta iṭaṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷ kippaṟṟik koṇṭu vāṇipam ceytu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷum mataveṟikoṇṭu, yāḻppāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil intuk kōyilkaḷi iṭittum, intukkaḷik kaṭṭāyappaṭuttik kiṟittavarkaḷākkiyum, kiṟittavarkaḷukku maṭṭum araciyal aluvalkaḷik koṭuttum koṭumi ceytaṉar. mutalil vāṇikarkaḷāka vanta ivarkaḷ piṟaku nāṭukaḷip piṭittu aracāḷa muṟpaṭṭārkaḷ. āṉāl, āṅkilēyar ivarkaḷi intiyāvilum ilaṅkiyilumiruntu turatti viṭṭārkaḷ.
intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikam ceytu celvan tiraṭṭa virumpiya maṟṟoru tēcattār āṅkilēyarāvar. ivarkaḷum ollāntarip pōlavē intiyāvukku vaṭakiḻakkup pātiyoṉṟik kaṇṭu piṭikka mutalil muyaṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ cumār 50 āṇṭukaḷāka vaṭakiḻakkup pātiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṟci ceytum payaṉaṭiya villi. piṟaku  1578-ām āṇṭil, car pirāṉcis ṭirēk13 eṉṉum āṅkilēyar, intiyāviliruntu pōrccukal tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟukoṇṭirunta oru paṟaṅkik kappalip piṭittuk koḷḷi yaṭittār. koḷḷiyiṭukiyil, āppirikkāviṉ naṉṉampikki muṉiyic cuṟṟik koṇṭu intiyāvukkuc cellum vaḻiyik kuṟippiṭukiṟa paṭam oṉṟik kaṇṭeṭuttār. ataṉpiṟaku 1594- l car jāṉ laṅkāsṭ14 eṉṉum āṅkilēyar naṉṉampikkimuṉi vaḻiyāka cāvā variyil yāttiri ceytu tirumpivantār. piṉṉar ki.pi.1600-ām āṇṭu ṭicampar mātam  “āṅkilēyak kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi” eṉṉum varttaka caṅkam niṟuvappaṭṭatu. atumutal āṅkilēyar intiyāvukku vantu vāṇikam ceyyat talippaṭṭaṉar.
namatu tēcattil vāṇikam ceyya vanta iṉṉoru airōppiya iṉattār ṭēṉs15 eṉṉum ṭēṉiṣkārar. ivarkaḷ ṭeṉmārkku tēcattic cērntavar 1620-l kiḻakkintiya varttaka caṅkatti ēṟpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, namatu tēcattil uḷḷa taraṅkampāṭi, paḻavēṟkāṭu, cirāmpūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceytuvantaṉar. āṉāl, ivarkaḷiṉ vāṇikam palappaṭavilli. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil, taraṅkampāṭi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷi ivarkaḷ āṅkilē yarukku viṟṟuviṭṭup pōyviṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷum kiṟittava matattip paravacceyya muyaṉṟu vantaṉar.
intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikam ceyyak kaṭiciyāka muyaṟci ceytavarkaḷ pireñcukkārar. “pireñcuk kiḻakkintiya varttakac caṅkam” 1664-l ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. pireñcukkārar putuccēri mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceytuvantaṉar. ṭiyupḷē eṉpavar putuccērikku aluvalāḷarāy vantapōtu intiyāvil pireñcu aracāṭciyi nilināṭṭa muyaṟciceytār. avaruṭiya muyaṟci veṟṟiyākavē iruntatu. āṉāl, pireñcukkārariṉ veṟṟiyiyum celvākkiyum taṭippaṭutti, avarkaḷi intiyāviliruntu turattiviṭṭu, āṅkila aracāṭciyi nilināṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇattuṭaṉ āṅkilēyak kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉiyār muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. piṟaku iru tiṟattārukkum pala pōrkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. kaṭiciyāka, āṅkilēyar veṟṟi peṟṟu āṅkila aracāṭciyi nili niṟuviṉar. itaṟku akkālattil intiyāviṉ nilimi iṭaṅ koṭuttatu. piṟaku, ilaṅki mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷiyum piṭittu aracāḷat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivaṟṟi yellām virivāka eḻutuvatu yām collap pukunta karuttukkup puṟampāṉatāku mātalāl, mikac curukkamākak kūṟappaṭṭatu.
vāṇikattukkāka vanta airōppiyarkaḷ intiyarik kiṟittava matattiṟ cērkkavum muyaṟci ceytu vantārkaḷ. intuk kaḷik kiṟittavarākkum poruṭṭu aṉēka pātirimārkaḷi airōppiya tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu aḻittu vantārkaḷ. namatu tēcattukku vanta pātirimārkaḷ kiṟittava matattip pōtikka muṟpaṭṭapōtu, avarkaḷukku innāṭṭumoḻikaḷ taṭiyāka niṉṟaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷ mutalil intiya moḻikaḷik kaṟkavēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam ēṟpaṭṭatu. tāṅkaḷ enta nāṭṭārukku matapōtaṉi ceyyak karutiṉārkaḷō anta nāṭṭu moḻiyik kaṟṟut tērntu, atiṟ pēcavum coṟpoḻivu ceyyavum muyaṉṟārkaḷ. anta muṟiyil tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vanta pātirimārkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyik kaṟṟut tērntu tamiḻiṟ pēcavum, coṟpoḻivu nikaḻttavum, nūl iyaṟṟavum toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivvāṟu tamiḻmoḻikkum airōppiyap pātiri mārukkum ēṟpaṭṭa toṭarpiṉāl, tamiḻiṟ cila māṟutalkaḷ allatu muṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟi ārāyvatu tāṉ ipputtakattiṉ nōkkamākum.
tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutal allatu vaḷarcci aṉittum pātirimārkaḷāl uṇṭāṉaviyē. illaṟattārākiya airōppiya uttiyōkattarkaḷāl tamiḻukku yātoṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭatākat teriyavilli. namatu nāṭṭil 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil irunta āṅkilēya aluvalāḷar taṅkaḷ aluval muṟikku utaviyāka iruppataṟkākavum, namatu nāṭṭuk kaliyiṉ pōkki aṟivataṟkākavum, “ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkam”16 eṉṉum oru caṅkam ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭu, ataṉ mūlamākat tamiḻik kaṟṟu vantārkaḷ. ic caṅkattiṉāl tamiḻmoḻikkuc ciṟappāka yātoru vaḷarcciyum uṇṭāṉatākac collamuṭiyātu. kiṟittavarkaḷāl tamiḻukku ēṟpaṭṭa apivirutti ellām pātirimāric cērntatē. pātirimārum tamiḻukkākac ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttuṭaṉ ceyyavilli. taṅkaḷ matattip parappuvataṟkākac ceyta muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉē tamiḻiṟkuc cila naṉmikaḷi aḷittatu. evvāṟāyiṉum, anta naṉmikaḷ airōppiyap pātirimārkaḷāl uṇṭāṉaviyē. avaṟṟi iṉi ārāyvōm.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1. urōmar, kirēkkar.				
2. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam.
3. Augustus Caesar.				
4. Strabo.		
5. Hippalus.
6. Emanuel.
7. Milanda.			
8. Calicuti.	
9. Zamorian.
10. Pedro Alvarez Cabral.		
11. Brazil.
12. Holland.
13. Sir Francis Drake.			
14. Sir John Lancaster.
15. Danes.
16. The Madras College.

2. urinaṭi nūl varaṉmuṟi
airōppiyar varuvataṟku muṉṉē tamiḻmoḻiyil taṉi urinaṭinūl iruntatilli. ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam, nikaṇṭu, cōtiṭam, maruttuvam mutaliya ellā nūlkaḷum ceyyuḷilē iyaṟṟappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ivikaḷukku uriyāciriyarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya urikaḷit tavira taṉippaṭṭa urinaṭinūl  oṉṟēṉum iruntatilli. āṉāl, paṇṭik kālattil urinaṭiyum ceyyuḷumākak kalantu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati,
	“toṉmi tāṉē,
uriyoṭu puṇarnta paḻimi mēṟṟē”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷil  238-vatu cūttirattāl aṟiyalām. avaṟṟi “uriyiṭi yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ” eṉpar. peruntēvaṉār pāratamum, takaṭūryāttiriyum uriyiṭi yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷukku utāraṇaṅkaḷākum. āṉāl, avviru nūlkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ippōtu, urinaṭiyum ceyyuḷumākak kalantu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūlukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭuvatu cilappatikāram oṉṟē. cilappatikāram uriyiṭiyiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ eṉpati,
	“uriyiṭi yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ
urikā laṭikaḷ aruḷa maturik
kūla vāṇikaṉ cāttaṉ kēṭṭaṉaṉ”
eṉṉum cilappatikārap patikac ceyyuḷaṭikaḷāl aṟiyalām. cilappatikāram uriyiṭi yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa pōtilum, annūlil mikac ciṟu pakuti maṭṭun tāṉ (uripeṟu kaṭṭuriyum vāḻttuk kātiyiṉ urippāṭṭu maṭiyum) urinaṭi; maṟṟap perum pakutikaḷ pāṭṭē. eṉavē, uriyum pāṭṭumākak kalantu ceyyappaṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟa cilappatikārattil, arikkāṟ pakutikūṭa urinaṭi aminti rukkavilli. iṟantupaṭṭa peruntēvaṉār pāratamum, takaṭūr yāttiriyum pōka, vaḻakkāṟṟil uḷḷa cilappatikāram oṉṟiṉit tavirttu, “uriyiṭi yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ” nūl tamiḻil vēṟoṉṟum illi. eṉavē, taṉi urinaṭi nūlkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭavilli eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
tamiḻt tollāciriyarkaḷ pāṭṭiyē peritum pōṟṟic ceyyuḷnaṭiyilēyē ellā nūlkaḷiyum iyaṟṟivittaṉar eṉpatu uṇmi. itaṉāl, paṇṭi āciriyarkaḷukku urinaṭi eḻutat teriyātu eṉṟu niṉikkakkūṭātu. avarkaḷ ciṟanta urinaṭiyil nūl iyaṟṟum āṟṟal vāyntavar eṉpatiṟ caṟṟum aiyamilli. iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷiṉ urippāyiram oṉṟē, avarkaḷ urinaṭi eḻutuvatil taliciṟantavar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarum. iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷiṉ urippāyira urinaṭiyiṉ aḻakiyum iṉimiyiyum paṭittu iṉpuṟāta tamiḻar uṇṭō? attakiya tīñcuvi coṭṭum urinaṭiyiṉi iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya paṇṭit tamiḻar, taṉi urinaṭi nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟa niṉittirup pārāṉāl, eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷi eḻutiyirukkak kūṭumaṉṟō? taṉi urinaṭi nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟuvatu akkālattil vaḻakkamillāta paṭiyāl, avarkaḷ urinaṭi nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟāmal, ellā nūlkaḷiyum ceyyuḷilē ceytu vittaṉar. (srīpurāṇam mutalāṉa, camaṇa camayattavarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷ uḷa. avi tamiḻ eṉṟum collakkūṭāmal camakkirutam eṉṟum collakkūṭāmal maṇippiravāḷa naṭiyāka iruppatāl, avaṟṟit tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ eṉṟu yām koḷḷavilli.) iṅṅaṉam paṇṭit tamiḻar ceyyuḷ naṭiyil maṭṭum nūl iyaṟṟi, urinaṭiyil nūl iyaṟṟuvati ēṉ puṟakkaṇittaṉar? urinaṭi eḻuta naṉkaṟintiruntum, paṇṭik kālattil urinaṭi nūl eḻutum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭāta kāraṇam eṉṉa? avaṟṟi ārāyvōm.
ikkālattil varuttamiṉṟi eḻutuvataṟkēṟṟa karuvikaḷum poruḷkaḷum ērāḷamāka amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. kākitam, pēṉā, peṉcil mutaliya karuvikaḷ eḻutuvōrukku yātoru varutta miṉṟi utaviyāy niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, paṇṭikkālattil eḻutukaruvikaḷ ivvaḷavu tuṇiyāy amintirukkavilli. paṉiyēṭukaḷum, iruppeḻuttāṇiyun tavira, vēṟu eḻutu karuvikaḷ akkālattil iruntatilli. paṉiyēṭukaḷil eḻuttāṇiyāl eḻutuvatu, kākitattiṟ pēṉāviṉāl eḻutuvatu pōl eḷitāṉa kāriyamaṉṟu; mikak kaṭiṉamāṉa kāriyam. ākiyāl, paṇṭittamiḻar eḻuttu vēliyi evvaḷavu kuṟikka vēṇṭumō avvaḷavum kuṟikka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. ākavē, ‘curuṅkac collal’ eṉṉum muṟiyik kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. uri naṭiyil nūl iyaṟṟuvatu eḻuttu vēliyik kuṟippatākātu; nērmāṟāka av vēliyi mikutip paṭuttuvatākum. ataṉāl tāṉ curukkamākac colluvataṟkēṟṟa ceyyuḷnaṭiyi nam paṇṭiyōr mēṟkoṇṭārkaḷ pōlum. iṉṉoru kāraṇamum uṇṭu. aḵteṉṉaveṉṟāl, akkālattil accup poṟiyum ataṉ payaṉākiya accupputtakamum illāta paṭiyāl, nūlkaḷik kaṟkum māṇavar tām kaṟkum ellā nūlkaḷiyum maṉappāṭañ ceyyavēṇṭi yiruntatōṭu, nūlkaḷip pāṭañ collum āciriyarkaḷum tām pōtikkum nūlkaḷi maṉappāṭañ ceytirukka vēṇṭiya avaciyam ēṟpaṭṭiruntatu. maṉappāṭam paṇṇuvataṟku urinaṭi ēṟṟataṉṟu; ceyyuḷ naṭitāṉ ciṟantatu. ikkāraṇat tiṉālum, paṇṭit tamiḻar nūl iyaṟṟuvataṟkuc ceyyuḷ naṭiyi mēṟkoṇṭu, uri naṭiyik kiviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu niṉikka iṭamuṇṭākiṟatu.
ikkālattuḷḷatu pōla akkālattil eḷitāka eḻutuvataṟ kēṟṟa eḻutukaruvikaḷum poruḷkaḷum illāta paṭiyiṉālē, eḻuttu vēliyic curukkik koḷvataṟkākap paṇṭiyā ciriyar ceyyuḷ naṭiyi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu coṉṉōm. ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyē uriyāciriyar kūṭa cila iṭaṅkaḷil, “itaṉi vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka” eṉṟu eḻutip pōntaṉar eṉṟu niṉikkiṟōm. kuṟippuri, arumpatavuri pōṉṟa curukka māṉa urikaḷip paṇṭiyōr eḻutat tuṇintataṟkuk kāraṇamum eḻutukaruvi tuṇipporuḷkaḷ illāk kuṟiyeṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ muṭimaṇiyāka viḷaṅkum cilappatikārattiṟku uriyeḻutiya āciriyar aṭiyārkkunallār, mēṟpaṭi nūṟpatikattiṉ mutal iraṇṭaṭikaḷukku maṭṭum mika virivāṉa uri eḻutiya piṉṉar, “iṉip patikac ceyyuḷuḷ, mutalīraṭiyum pōla yāṇṭum viriyātatu uriperuku meṉpatik karuti eṉak koḷka,” eṉa eḻuti, maṟṟa aṭikaḷukku uriyic curukkik koṇṭatum iṅku nōkkat takkatu. uri eḻutuvataṟkeṉṟē muṟpaṭṭavar, piṉ uri peruku meṉṟu karutic curukkik koṇṭatu, culapamāka eḻutuvataṟku ēṟṟa karuvikaḷ akkālattu illāmiyā leṉka. ikkālattuḷḷatu pōṉṟa vacatiyāṉa eḻutukaruvikaḷ akkālattu iruntiruntāl ivvāṟu uriyic curukkik koṇṭirukka māṭṭāraṉṟō? ākavē, paṇṭittamiḻar urinaṭi nūl iyaṟṟātatu ak kālattil vacatiyāṉa eḻutukaruvikaḷum accu yantiramum illāta kāraṇam paṟṟiyē eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
paṇṭi nāḷil tamiḻmoḻiyil urinaṭi nūlkaḷ iruntirukkumāṉāl, tamiḻnāṭu pala tuṟikaḷilum iṉṉum muṉṉēṟṟamaṭintirukkum, ēṉeṉṟāl, oru nāṭu muṉṉēṟṟa maṭiya vēṇṭumāṉāl, annāṭṭil urinaṭi nūlkaḷ uṇṭāka vēṇṭum. mēl nāṭukaḷil neṭu nāṭkaḷukku muṉpē urinaṭi nūlkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruntapaṭiyāl, annāṭṭu makkaḷ pala ceytikaḷiyum eḷitil uṇarntu, virivil muṉṉēṟṟamaṭiya muṭintatu. ik karuttiyē nītipatiyum ciṟanta tamiḻk kaviyumāyirunta māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ,
“vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷāl makkaḷ tirunta vēṇṭumēyallātu ceyyuṭkaḷip paṭittut tiruntuvatu acāttiyam aṉṟō? airōppiya moḻikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāma lirukku māṉāl, antat tēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum naṟpāṅkum aṭin tirukkak kūṭumā? appaṭiyē nammuṭiya tāymoḻiyil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkiṟa variyil, intattēcam cariyāṉa cīrtiruttam aṭiyāteṉpatu niccayam.”
eṉṟu tām eḻutiya ‘piratāpa mutaliyār’ carittirattil eḻuti yirukkiṟār. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil perum pulavarāy viḷaṅkiya tiruttaṇiki vicākap perumāḷiyar eṉṉum vīracivar tām eḻutiya ‘pāla pōta ilakkaṇam’ eṉṉum nūliṉ pāyirattil, urinaṭi nūlkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyip paṟṟik kīḻ varumāṟu eḻutukiṟār:
“ilakkaṇa ilakkiya kaṇita pūkōḷa kakōḷāti nūlkaḷi yellām, ikkālattu ittēcatti yāḷukiṉṟa iṅkilīṣkārar kaḷum, avarkaḷ vacikkum kaṇṭattiluḷḷa ēṉiyōrkaḷum, ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟik kaṟpippati viṭṭu, vacaṉaṅkaḷilē teḷivuṟa iyaṟṟic ciṟuvarkkuk kaṟpittukkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl, ak kaṇṭattil vāḻum ciṟuvarkaḷ cila nāṭkaḷilē pala nūlkaḷik kaṟṟup pala viṣayaṅkaḷiyum uṇarntu pala toḻilkaḷiyum iyaṟṟum tiṟamuṭiyarākiṉṟaṉar.
“ittēcattār annūlkaḷi yellām ceyyuḷilē ceytu avaṟṟiṟkut tiricoṟkaḷāl uriyiyaṟṟic ciṟuvarkkuk kaṟpittu varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ, nikaṇṭu mutaliya karuvi nūlkaḷik kaṟṟalaṉṟik kaṟkappaṭāvām. ākavē, avaṟṟuḷ oru nūlik kaṟpataṟku neṭu nāḷ  cellukiṉṟatu. cellavē, pala nūlkaḷik kaṟṟuṇarntu ettoḻilkaḷiyuñ ceyyattakkavarkaḷāvatu aritām.
“itaṉāl innāṭṭuc ciṟuvarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻnāṭkaḷ vīḻ nāṭ paṭāmaṟ cila nāṭkaḷil ap pala nūlkaḷiyum kaṟṟaṟintu et toḻilkaḷiyum ceyyattakka vallami aṭivataṟku, an nūlkaḷiyellām ulakavaḻakkiyaṟ coṟkaḷāl urivacaṉamākat terivuṟac ceytu kalviccālikaḷilē upayōkamuṟum paṭi ceytal naṉṟu....”
mēlē kāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷkaḷiṉāl, nāṭu nākarikam peṟṟu nalamaṭivataṟku urinaṭi nūlkaḷē pērutaviyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, mēlittēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum celvamum peṟṟut tikaḻvataṟku an nāṭukaḷil vacaṉa nūlkaḷ vaḻakkāṟṟiliruntu vantatum oru kāraṇam eṉpatum teḷivākiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, urinaṭi nūlkaḷiṉ payaṉ naṉku teḷivākiṟatu, iṉi, tamiḻ moḻiyil urinaṭi nūl uṇṭāṉa varalāṟṟiṉi ārāyvōm.
ettoḻilum paḻakkattiṉāṟ ciṟappaṭikiṟatu. ik karuttup paṟṟiyē, “ cittiramum kip paḻakkam centamiḻum nāppaḻakkam” eṉṉum āṉṟōr moḻi eḻuntatu. ippaḻamoḻi uri naṭikkum poruntiyatē. pallāṇṭu pallāṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻar urinaṭi nūl eḻutāmalē iruntu viṭṭapaṭiyāl, kālap pōkkil urinaṭi eḻutum āṟṟali iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. tamiḻariṉ ik kuṟipāṭṭiṉip paṟṟi airōppiyar cilar kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. “tamiḻil urinaṭi eḻutum paḻakkam iṉṉum tuvakkaniliyil irukkiṟatu. virivākavum caramāriyākavum tamiḻiṟ kavipāṭakkūṭiya pulavarkaḷ urinaṭiyiṟ cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷ eḻutat teriyāmalirukkiṉṟaṉar,” eṉṉum karuttuppaṭa uviṉslō tamiḻ-iṅkilīṣ akarātiyiṉ mukavuriyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. “maruttuvam, kaṇitam, ilakkaṇam, nikaṇṭu mutaliya ellā nūlkaḷum (avaṟṟiṉ urikaḷit tavira) ceyyuḷilē iyaṟṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. uri naṭiyil nūl iyaṟṟum vaḻakkam airōppiyariṉ toṭarpāl ēṟpaṭṭatākum” eṉṟu tamiḻippaṟṟi marṭāk turi eḻutiyirukkiṟār. ivi uṇmiyē, tamiḻil maṭṭumaṉṟu; ellā intiya moḻi kaḷilum urinaṭi vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. airōppiyariṉ toṭar puṇṭāṉa piṟakutāṉ eṉpatiṟ caṟṟēṉum aiyamilli.
tamiḻil taṉi urinaṭi nūl airōppiyarāl mutal mutal uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmi. eppōtu yārāl, eṉṉa urinaṭi nūl mutal mutal uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu eṉpati iṅku ārāyvōm. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ mutaṟpakutiyil, tamiḻnāṭṭil vantu vāḻntirunta ittāli tēcattavarāṉa peski eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar tām tamiḻil mutaṉmutal urinaṭinūl iyaṟṟiṉavar eṉpatu perum pāṉmi yōr karuttu. perumpāṉmiyōr karuttu eṉṟālum, itu tavaṟāṉa muṭipākum. vīramāmuṉivarukku muṉṉarē, patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil, tamiḻ nāṭṭil vantu vāḻntirunta ittāliya tēcattavarāṉa irāparṭ nopili eṉṉum tattuvapōtaka cuvāmi pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷit tamiḻil iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟār. vīramāmuṉivarippaṟṟit terintiruppatu pōla, tattuvapōtaka cuvāmiyippaṟṟip palarukkut teriyātu. vīramāmuṉivarukku muṉṉarē ivar tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟār. eṉṟālum, ivarit “tamiḻ urinūliṉ tanti” eṉṟu colvataṟ killi. ēṉeṉṟāl, ivarukkum muṉṉarē, patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ uṇṭāyiruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ki.pi.1577ām āṇṭiltāṉ tamiḻil mutaṉ mutal urinaṭi nūl uṇṭāṉatākat terikiṟatu. inta nūlukkuk “kiṟittava vētōpatēcam”1 eṉpatu peyar. ēcuviṉ capip pātirimārāl eḻuti acciṭappaṭṭa inta mutal urinaṭi nūl ippōtu intiyāvil illi eṉṟu terikiṟatu. oruvēḷi airōppāk kaṇṭattil uḷḷa puttakacālikaḷiṟ kiṭikkakkūṭum. inta nūli yār iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu teriyavilli. cavēriyār eṉṉum ceyṉṭ sēviyar eḻutiṉār eṉṟu cilar collukiṟārkaḷ. itu tavaṟu. ēṉeṉṟāl, cavēriyārukkut tamiḻ eḻutap paṭikkat teriyātu. ākiyāl, inta mutal tamiḻ urinaṭi nūli avar iyaṟṟiyirukka muṭiyātu. inta nūl acciṭṭa piṟaku, 1579-il “kiṟittava vaṇakkam”2 eṉṉum nūl acciṭappaṭṭatu. inta urinaṭi nūli eḻutiyavar āṉṟik3 eṉṉum ēcuviṉ capiyic cērnta pātiriyār eṉṟu terikiṟatēyaṉṟi, avarippaṟṟiya vēṟu varalāṟu oṉṟum teriyavilli. inta nūlum namatu tēcattil ippōtu kiṭikkavilli. pāris nakarap puttaka cāliyil intap puttakattiṉ piratiyoṉṟu iruppatāka uṟutiyākat terikiṟatu.
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, mēṟcoṉṉa iraṇṭu nūlkaḷit tavira, ēcuviṉ capiyiccērnta airōppiyap pātirimār tamiḻ kaṟpataṟkākac cila nūlkaḷ acciṭap paṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, an nūlkaḷip paṟṟi oṉṟum teriyavilli. tamiḻil mutal urinaṭi nūl uṇṭāṉatu 1577-il eṉpatu maṭṭum uṟutiyākat terikiṟatu.
16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟaku, mēlē colliyapaṭi  17-ām  nūṟṟāṇṭil, rāpaṭ nopili eṉṉum tattuva pōtaka cuvāmi cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār. inta nūṟṟāṇṭil ivarit tavira, aruḷāṉantar eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa jāṉ-ṭi-piriṭṭō pātiriyār cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷit tamiḻil eḻutiyiruppatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl aruḷāṉantacuvāmi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ emakku ituvari kiṭikkavilli. tattuva pōtaka cuvāmi iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷip paṟṟi maṟṟōriṭattil avar varalāṟṟiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm.
ataṉ piṟaku,18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, peski eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟār. ivar kālattilē, tañcik kaṭutta taraṅkam pāṭiyil vāḻntirunta cīkaṉ pālku mutalāṉa cermaṉi tēcattup pātirimārkaḷum cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. itē nūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ tiruvāvaṭutuṟi ātīṉattic cērnta mātavac civañāṉa cuvāmiyum cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiṉār. civañāṉa cuvāmiyiṉ urinaṭi vīramāmuṉivariṉ uri naṭiyip pōṉṟu mikacciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. cermaṉi tēcattavarāṉa cīkaṉ pālku aiyar eḻutiya urinaṭi potumakkaḷukku ēṟṟa koccittamiḻnaṭiyil amintatu. vīramāmuṉivar, cīkaṉpālku aiyar ivarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟiṉi maṟṟōr iṭattiṟ kāṇka.
tamiḻurinaṭinūl mutalmutal tōṉṟiyatu 16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eṉṟālum, atu vērūṉṟi nili peṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ. 17,18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻ uri naṭi nūlkaḷ atikamāka ēṟpaṭa villi. 1577-il mutal urinaṭi nūl uṇṭāṉataṟkup piṟaku, irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ variyilum, tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarcci peṟāmalē kuṉṟiyiruntatu. ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ ērāḷamāka veḷippaṭat toṭaṅkiṉa. itu viyapputtāṉ. irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka urinaṭi vaḷarcci taṭippaṭṭiruntu, piṟaku tiṭīreṉṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyateṉṟāl, taṭippaṭṭiruntataṟkum, piṟaku tiṭīreṉṟu vaḷarntataṟkum kāraṇamirukkavēṇṭumaṉṟō? akkāraṇantāṉ yātu? kāraṇam araciyaṟ kuḻappaṅkaḷē. 16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil, namatu nāṭṭu varalāṟṟip puraṭṭip pārttāl itu naṉku viḷaṅkum, paṇṭiya tamiḻ maṉṉarum ciṟṟaracarum aracāṭci iḻantu, putiya putiya aracarkaḷum talivarkaḷum innāṭṭil tōṉṟiṉar. piṟaku avarum aḻintoḻintu, kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷum pāḷiyakkārarkaḷum aracāṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kālattil valiyavaṉ meliyavaṉi aṭittup piṭuṅkuvatu eṉkiṟa muṟi nāṭeṅkum naṭipeṟṟu vantatu. pireñcu, iṅkiliṣ kampeṉikkāra caṇṭikaḷum, karnāṭaka navāppukaḷiṉ pōrkaḷum, pāḷiyakkārariṉ tollikaḷum, marāṭṭiyariṉ paṭiyeṭuppum, aitarāli tippu cultāṉ kalakaṅkaḷum ākiya araciyaṟkuḻappaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, nāṭṭumakkaḷip paṭātapāṭu paṭuttiviṭṭaṉa. nili peṟṟa aracāṭciyum, uyirukkum poruḷukkum pātukāppum illā miyālum, mēṟcoṉṉa kuḻappaṅkaḷiṉ kāraṇattālum, makkaḷ kalviyiṟ karuttuc celuttiṉārkaḷilli. āṉālum, kāriruṭṭil miṉmiṉi tōṉṟuvatu pōl, intak kuḻappak kālattilum camayat toṇṭāṟṟi vanta kiṟittuvap pātirikaḷum. civa maṭātipatikaḷumē oruvāṟu kalviyiṟ karuttic celutta muṭintatu. icciṟupāṉmi yōrākiya camaya ūḻiyarkaḷilum oru cilar tām urinaṭinūl iyaṟṟa muṉvantaṉar. viralviṭṭeṇṇak kūṭiyapaṭi mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa nālintu urinaṭi nūlāciriyarkaḷit tavira, vēṟu āciriyarkaḷ 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari ēṟpaṭavilli.
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil araciyaṟ kuḻappaṅkaḷum caṇṭikaḷum oḻintu, āṅkila aracāṅkam nilipeṟṟu, uyirukkum poruḷukkum pātukāppum, tēcattil amitiyum, oḻuṅkum ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, iyaṟkiyākavē makkaḷ kalviyil karuttic celuttiṉārkaḷ. urinaṭi nūlkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyi uṇarntu, urinaṭi nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, innūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ pātiri mārkaḷālum aracāṅkat tārālum tēcameṅkum pāṭacālikaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭṭavarkaḷāl naṭattappaṭṭa pāṭacālikaḷukku aracāṅkattār poruḷutaviceytu ūkkappaṭuttiṉar. ivvāṟu āṅkāṅkē pāṭacālikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa paṭiyālum, ippāṭacālikaḷil airōppiya muṟippaṭi ilakkiyam, pūkōḷam, carittiram mutaliya ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum urinaṭip pāṭamākak kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkiyapaṭiyālum, tamiḻurinaṭinūlkaḷ ērāḷamāka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. iḵtaṉṟiyum, inta nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ accup poṟikaḷ nāṭu muḻutum āṅkāṅkē niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. aracāṅka aluvalkaḷil amarntirunta airōppiya uyartara aluvalāḷarkaḷum, camayat toṇṭāṟṟivanta airōppiya pātirimārum tamiḻmoḻiyik kaṟkavēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam akkālattil ēṟpaṭṭiruntapaṭiyāl, avarkaḷ eḷitākat tamiḻik kaṟkum poruṭṭu urinaṭi nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭu acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. “miṣaṉ” eṉṉum kiṟittuvac caṅkaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅkē niṟuvappaṭṭu, ac caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka matac cārpāṉa urinaṭi nūlkaḷ ērāḷamāka acciṭappaṭṭuk kuṟinta vilikkuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṉṟiyum, ac caṅkaṅkaḷ urinaṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭa tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷi acciṭṭu viliyiṉṟi makkaḷukku vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. itaṉ vāyilākavum urinaṭinūlkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ parava vaḻiyuṇṭāyiṟṟu. pātirimārkaḷ ceytuvarum ittakiya camaya ūḻiyattikkaṇṭu, itukāṟum ikkāriyattil uṟaṅkikkiṭanta intukkaḷ viḻippaṭintu, intu mataccārpāṉa nūlkaḷiyum purāṇa itikācaṅkaḷiyum urinaṭi nūlkaḷāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, inta nūṟṟāṇṭil, kiṟittuva matattārukkum, intu matattārukkum, civarukkum viṇavarukkum, mataccārpāṉa vaḻakku etirvaḻakkukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa paṭiyāl, avaravarkaḷ tattam matattip pukaḻntum ayalār matattik kuṟi kūṟiyum, urinaṭip puttakaṅkaḷiyum, tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷiyum eḻuti acciṭṭu vaḻaṅki vantaṉar.
ivi oru puṟamirukka, 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kalvi 
vaḷarcciyi muṉṉiṭṭuc cila caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi, tamiḻ uṭinaṭi nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭa utavipurintaṉa. `ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkam’4 eṉṉum oru capi ēṟpaṭṭu, pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷiyum maṟṟum cila urinaṭip puttakaṅkaḷiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭatu. ‘ceṉṉip pāṭacālip puttakac caṅkam’5 eṉṉum caṅkam 1850-il ēṟpaṭṭup pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭu utaviṟṟu. intac capiyōr nalla urinaṭi nūl eḻutuvōrukkup poruḷ utaviceytu, paricaḷittu vantaṉar. ‘irāpiṉcaṉ kurūcō,’ ‘intiya carittiram,’ ‘ulaka carittiram’ mutaliya ciṟanta moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷ iccaṅkattār aḷitta paricu kāraṇamāka veḷi vantavi. akkālattil veḷivanta ciṟanta urinaṭi nūlkaḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum acciṭap paṭāmiyāl ippōtu maṟintuviṭṭaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil aracāṇṭa kiḻakkintiya aracāṅkattār, ‘naṉṉūl’ ilakkaṇatti urinaṭiyāka eḻutuvōrukku vekumati yaḷippatāka viḷamparam ceytārkaḷ. ataṉ payaṉākattāṉ tamiḻil urinaṭi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iviyaṉṟiyum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ ceytit tāḷkaḷum6 māta vāra veḷiyīṭukaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil tōṉṟi ulāvat toṭaṅkiṉa. ivvāṟu paṟpala vaḻikaḷil tamiḻ urinaṭi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ nāṭṭil vērūṉṟic ceḻittōṅki vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṉa.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
