3. accup puttaka varalāṟu
iṉṟiya ulakam muṉṉēṟṟamaṭintu, nākarikam peṟṟuc ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruppavikaḷuḷ accup puttakamum oṉṟākum. kākitamum accup poṟiyum ēṟpaṭṭu, accup puttakam uṇṭāṉa piṟaku tāṉ, kalvi eṉṉum aṟivuoḷi nāṭeṅkum parava vaḻiyuṇṭāyiṟṟu. accup puttakam varuvataṟku muṉṉē paṉi ēṭu, pataṉiṭṭa tōl mutaliya poruḷkaḷil makkaḷ nūlkaḷi eḻuti vantaṉar. ōli mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ puttakam eḻutuvatu kaṭiṉamāṉa kāriyam. oru puttakam eḻuti muṭippataṟkut tēka uḻippu orupuṟa mirukka, kālattiyum poruḷiyum atikamākac celavu ceyya vēṇṭi yiruntatu. ākiyāl, paṇṭik kālattil, poruḷ uḷḷavar maṭṭum puttakam peṟṟu aṟivi aṭiya vacatiyiruntatu; ēṉiya peruntokiyarāṉa ēḻi makkaḷ puttakam vāṅka vacatiyillāmalē yiruntaṉar. ik kāraṇattiṉāl, accup puttakam varuvataṟku muṉṉē, kalvi eṉṉum cuṭaroḷi, puttakam peṟa vacatiyuḷḷa ciṟupāṉmi yōriṭattil,--mikamikak kuṟivāṉa oru cilariṭattil maṭṭum, miṉmiṉipōla oḷiviṭṭuk koṇṭirunta tēyaṉṟi, nāṭṭi luḷḷa ellā makkaḷiṭattilum avvaṟivoḷi parava vacatiyillāmalē iruntatu. accuppoṟi vanta piṟaku, kuṟinta celavil, ciṟu uḻippil, kuṟukiya nērattil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭuvatāl, tōṭṭi mutal toṇṭimāṉ variyil, nāṭṭumakkaḷ aṉivarum puttakaṅkaḷik kuṟinta vilikku vāṅkippaṭittu, aṟivippeṟa utaviyāyirukkiṟatu. tēca makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattiṟ paṭintirunta kallāmi eṉṉum kāriruṭ paṭalam, accup puttakam vanta piṟaku, cūriyaṉik kaṇṭa paṉipōl maṟiyat toṭaṅ kiṟṟu. potumakkaḷukkum puttakam vāṅka vacati ēṟpaṭṭa piṟakutāṉ, kalvi kaṟṟu mēṉmēlum aṟivip perukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum āci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. accup puttakam vantataṉāl potuvāka ulakattil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutal ituvē.
accup puttakam varuvataṟku muṉṉē, nam nāṭṭup potu makkaḷum nūlkaḷip paṭittu aṟivip perukka vacatiyillāmal iruntaṉar. oru ciṟu tokiyiṉarākiya paṭitta kūṭṭattār paṉi yōlikaḷil nūlkaḷi eḻuti vantaṉar. iruppeḻuttāṇi koṇṭu paṉiyōlikaḷil nūlkaḷi eḻutimuṭikkat tēka uḻippum, kālac celavum poruṭcelavum atikamāka ēṟpaṭṭatu orupakkamirukka, avaṟṟip paṭippatum kaṭiṉamākaviruntatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, cuvaṭikaḷi vacatiyākak kiyāḷa muṭiyāta varuttattōṭu paṭikka muyaṉṟālum, ēṭukaḷil mūlam iṉṉatu, uri iṉṉatu eṉpatu teriyāmalum, puḷḷi peṟavēṇṭiya eḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟāmalum orē eḻuttu mayamākak kāṇappaṭum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka kaḷvar eṉṟu ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟateṉṟu vittuk koḷvōm. itik kaḷavā eṉṟu paṭippatā kaḷvar eṉṟu paṭippatā eṉkiṟa aiyam virivil tīrātu. ivvitam ēṭukaḷil meyyeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷi peṟātiruppatiṉāl uṇṭākum aiyappāṭukaḷ orupuṟamirukka muṟṟuppuḷḷi, kāṟpuḷḷi, arip puḷḷikaḷum kāṇappaṭā. ivaṟṟi ellāmviṭa mikavum varuttan tarum vēṟoṉṟu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, ōliccuvaṭikaḷiṟ pala vēṟu pāṭukaḷum iṭiccerukalkaḷum kāṇappaṭuvatu tāṉ. accup puttaṅkaḷilō iṭiccerukalum pāṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷum nuḻivataṟ kiṭamilli. nuḻikkavum muṭiyātu. ōliccuvaṭikaḷil ivi ilēcāka nuḻivataṟku iṭamuṇṭu. eppaṭi eṉṟāl, ōr āciriyaṉ oru nūli eḻutiṉāṉeṉṟāl, annūli maṟṟavarkaḷ paṭi eḻutikkoḷvatu paṇṭiya vaḻakkam. ippaṭip paṭikaḷ eḻutumpōtu, avaṟṟi eḻutuvōrkkut terintō teriyā malō pāṭavēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭuvatum uṇṭu. pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷiṉāl nulāciriyaṉ karuttukku māṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷum annūlūṭ pukuntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṭeḻutuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmiyiṉālum, koḷki vēṟupāṭṭiṉālum maṟṟum piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷālum, pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruppati ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiltāṉ kāṇamuṭiyum. ippoḻutu tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa kamparāmāyaṇa ēṭṭuppaṭikaḷik koṇṭu vantu avaṟṟi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu oppiṭṭup pārppōmāṉāl, ovvoru cuvaṭiyilum vevvēṟu pāṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷ iruppatik kāṇalām. ivvakiyē maṟṟa nūṟcuvaṭikaḷiyum oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu oppiṭṭup pārppōmāṉāl, avaṟṟilum pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷiyāvatu iṭiccerukal kaḷiyāvatu kāṇamuṭiyum. ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷ ellām orē pāṭa muḷḷaviyā yiruppatu arumi. tirukkuṟaḷukku uriyeḻutiya maṇakkuṭavar, tirukkuṟaḷ 7-ām atikārattiṟku “makkaṭpēṟu” eṉṉum talippeyar koṭuttirukka, maṟṟoru uriyāciriyarākiya parimēlaḻakar atē atikārattiṟkup “putalvarip peṟutal” eṉṉum talippeyar iṭṭiruppatu yāvarum aṟintatē. iḵtu, āṇmakkaḷukkum peṇ makkaḷukkum cama urimiyuṇṭu eṉṉum maṇakkuṭavar karuttiyum, peṇmakkaḷukku āṇmakkaḷippōl cama urimi kūṭātu eṉṉum parimēlaḻakar koḷkiyiyum naṉku viḷakkuvatōṭu, ēṭeḻutuvōr allatu uriyiṭuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmikkum matak koḷkikkum ēṟpa nūlkaḷiṟ pāṭavēṟupāṭu amikkappaṭum eṉpataṟkuc ciṟanta cāṉṟākavum niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvaki ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiṟ pala kuṟipāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvaki kuṟipāṭukaḷum iṭarppāṭukaḷum accup puttakattiṟ kāṇappaṭā. niṟka.
iṉi, tamiḻil accup puttakam vanta varalāṟṟiṉi ārāyvōm. namatu nāṭṭil airōppiyar vanta piṟaku tāṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. namatu nāṭṭil kiṟittuva matattip paravacceyvataṟkākap perum toṇṭāṟṟi vanta ēcuviṉ capip pātirimārkaḷ mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil accupputtakam uṇṭāk kiṉārkaḷ. intiya moḻikaḷil mutaṉ mutal accup puttakam uṇṭāṉatu tamiḻmoḻiyilētāṉ. piṟakutāṉ ēṉiya intiya moḻikaḷil accup puttakam uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
namatu nāṭṭil tamiḻ accup puttakam uṇṭāṉa varalāṟṟi ārāyvataṟku muṉṉam, airōppāk kaṇṭattil acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatu amivuṭittu. āṉāl, airōppāk kaṇṭattil acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻp puttakaṅ kaḷiṉ muḻu varalāṟu ippōtu emakkuk kiṭikkavilli. eṉiṉum, yāmaṟinta variyil eḻutuvōm. maliyāḷattut tāvara  nūl eṉṉum puttakam maliyāḷa tēcattil uḷḷa koṭi ceṭikaḷip paṟṟik kūṟum nūl. inta nūlil marañceṭikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ maṭṭum tamiḻil acciṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. innūl ollāntu tēcattiṉ1 talinakarāṉa āmsṭarṭām2 nakarattil 1686-l acciṭappaṭṭatu. innūlil marañceṭikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi maṭṭum kuṟippiṭum tamiḻ acceḻuttu annakarattil uṇṭākkap paṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. ataṉpiṟaku jermaṉi tēcattic cērnta hāli3 nakarattil,1780-ām āṇṭil, (tañcit taraṅkampāṭiyil irunta cīkaṉpālku aiyar eṉṉum ṭēṉiṣ miṣaṉ talivariṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi) tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaku, anta nakarattiṟ cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṟ patikkappaṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. ṣūls aiyar eṉṉum cermāṉiyar “paratīs tōṭṭam”4 eṉṉum nūlit tamiḻil moḻi peyarttu 1749-il acciṟ patippittār. “uṇmik kiṟittuvam”5 eṉṉum puttakatti “ñāṉak kaṇṇāṭi” eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ tamiḻil moḻipeyartteḻuti 1750-il acciṭṭār. ip puttakaṅkaḷ hāli nakarattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. (ṣūls aiyar tañcikkaṭutta taraṅkampāṭiyil vantu tamiḻ kaṟṟa pātiriyāvar.) iṉṉum cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ cermaṉi tēcattil 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil acciṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ ippōtu emakkuk kiṭikka villiyākiyāl, avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟṟi eḻuta iyalavilli. cermaṉi tēcattil tamiḻpputtakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭa atē kālattilō, ataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉ piṉṉākavō, ittāli tēcattic cērnta urōmā puriyilum tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷil, kiṟittuva matattip paravacceyyum poruṭṭu, kīḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṟ kiṟittuva mata nūlkaḷi acciṭuvataṟkāka accuppoṟi niliyam urōmā puriyil amikkappaṭṭirunta ceyti airōppiyar  eḻutiyuḷḷa paḻiya nūlkaḷiṉāl veḷippaṭukiṟatu. anta accup poṟi niliyattil intiya moḻikaḷiṟ cila nūlkaḷ acciṭap paṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl aṅku acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻpputta kaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ ippōtu aṟiyak kūṭāmalirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, namatu nāṭṭil mutaṉ mutal accup puttakam vanta varalāṟṟiṉi ārāyvōm. intiyāvilē, atilum tamiḻ moḻiyil, mutaṉ mutal accup puttakam uṇṭākkiya perumi ēcuviṉ capip pātirimāric cērntatu eṉṟu mēlē coṉ ṉōm. tamiḻ accup puttakavaralāṟṟippaṟṟit tiṭṭamākak kūṟum nūl oṉṟēṉum illi. ākiyāl, āṅkāṅkuc citaṟikkiṭakkum ceytikaḷiliruntum kuṟippukaḷiliruntum ivvaralāṟu oruvāṟu eḻutappaṭukiṟatu.
maliyāḷa tēcattil uḷḷa kocciyilum, ataṉ piṟaku cumār 1578-l, tirunelvēliyil uḷḷa puṉṉikkāyal eṉṉum iṭattilum, ēcuviṉ capi pātirimār6 accup poṟikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. piṟaku vippukkōṭṭi, ampalakkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum accuppoṟikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. inta accup poṟikaḷ yāvum16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyilē tāṉ amikkappaṭṭaṉa.
mutal accupputtakam ki.pi.1577-l acciṭappaṭṭatu. “kiṟittuva   vētōpatēcam”7 eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa apputtakam vippuk kōṭṭi eṉṉum ūril acciṭappaṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. jōṇ kōṇsālvas eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa, speyṉ tēcattavarākiya ēcuviṉ capip pātiriyār uṇṭākkiya acceḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭu ipputtakam acciṭappaṭṭatu. aṭuttapaṭiyāka,1579-l “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam”8 eṉṉum puttakam kocciyil acciṟ patikkappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭil pōrccukīs tamiḻp puttakam9 oṉṟum maliyāḷa tēcattil ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatu. iḵtu iṉṉāci āccāmaṇi10 eṉṉum cutēci oruvar uṇṭākkiya acceḻuttuk koṇṭu acciṭap paṭṭatu. aṉṟiyum, airōppiyap pātirimārkaḷ tamiḻ kaṟṟuk koḷḷuvataṟku utaviyākac cila puttakaṅkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa vākat terikiṉṟaṉa. mēṟ collappaṭṭa “kiṟittava vētōpatēcam,”  “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam”  eṉṉum iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷip paṟṟic cilarukku ōr aiyappāṭu uṇṭu. aḵteṉṉaveṉṟāl, ivviru puttakaṅkaḷum tamiḻmoḻiyil acciṭappaṭṭaṉavā, allatu maliyāḷa moḻiyil acciṭap paṭṭaṉavā eṉkiṟa aiyappāṭutāṉ. avi maliyāḷa moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭavi eṉṟu cilarum, tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭavi eṉṟu vēṟu cilarum karutukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl, ap puttakaṅkaḷ ippōtu intiyāviṟ kiṭiyā. mēṟpaṭip puttakaṅkaḷip paṟṟi eḻutiya airōppiyarkaḷ avi “malapār” moḻiyil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. “malapār” moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭavi eṉpataṉālum, maliyāḷa tēcattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭataṉālum, avi maliyāḷap puttakaṅ kaḷākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu oru kaṭciyār koḷki. ivarkaḷ niṉippatu cariyāṉateṉṟu tōṉṟalām. āṉāl, uḷ nuḻintu ārāyntu pārttāl, avarkaḷiṉ karuttut tavaṟāṉa teṉṟum, uṇmi avar karuttukku māṟāṉateṉṟum teriyavarum. maliyāḷa tēcattilē acciṭṭa paṭiyiṉālē apputtakaṅkaḷ maliyāḷa moḻiyiltāṉ irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉippatu tavaṟu. “malapār” moḻi eṉṟāl maliyāḷa moḻi eṉṟu karutuvatum tavaṟākum. ippoḻutu “malapār”  eṉkiṟa collukkup poruḷ vēṟu; cumār nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ic collukkirunta poruḷ vēṟu. intak kālattil “malapār” eṉṉum airōppiyac col maliyāḷa tēcattik kuṟikkiṟatu. āṉāl, itē “malapār” eṉṉum col cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻar allatu tamiḻ moḻi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅki vantatēyaṉṟi, ippōtu vaḻaṅkum poruḷik kuṟippiṭavilli. namatu nāṭṭil vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭu vantirunta palvēṟu airōppiya iṉattārum tamiḻari “malapārikaḷ” eṉṟum, tamiḻmoḻiyi “malapār moḻi” eṉṟum akkālattil vaḻaṅki vantati avarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkum nūlkaḷiliruntu ippōtum terintukoḷḷalām. ivar maṭṭumiṉṟi, ilaṅkiyi āṇṭirunta ollāntar mutaliya airōppiyarum ilaṅkit tamiḻari “malapārikaḷ” eṉṟu vaḻaṅkivantaṉar eṉpataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ pala nūlkaḷiliruntu kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkālattil ip peyar palarukku viyappiyuṇṭākkak kūṭum. āṉāl iḵtu uṇmi eṉpatu ārāyntu pārttāl viḷaṅkum.
airōppiyar tamiḻari “malapārikaḷ” eṉṟu ēṉ vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ? ippeyari avarkaḷ mukammatiyariṭattiliruntu kaṟṟatākat terikiṟatu. airōppiyar varuvataṟku muṉṉamē, mukammatiyar cilar teṉ intiyāvukku vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tamatu vaḻi naṭiyippaṟṟi eḻutiya puttakaṅkaḷil teṉ intiyāvi “māpār” eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppatākavum, piṟkālattil vanta airōppiyar “māpār” eṉṉum colli “malapār” eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. teṉ intiyāvik kuṟippiṭum “māpār” eṉṉum colli “malapār” ākki olitta airōppiyar teṉ intiyāvil vāḻum makkaḷiyum “malapār” eṉṟu vaḻaṅki ṉārkaḷ. piṟaku iccol, teṉ intiyāvil peruṅkuṭimakkaḷāka virukkum tamiḻarukkum, avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyākiya tamiḻukkum amintiruntatu. ippōtu accol paṇṭiya poruḷkaḷi utaṟiviṭṭu, maliyāḷa tēcattimaṭṭum kuṟikkiṟatu. “malapār” eṉṉum iccolliṉ varalāṟṟiṉi aṟiyātavar, ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkum poruḷil accolli vittu ārāyvatāl, uṇmi pulappaṭāmal iṭarppaṭukiṟārkaḷ. inta iṭarppāṭṭiṉāltāṉ mēṟcoṉṉa iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷum maliyāḷa moḻip puttakaṅka ḷeṉṟu avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ karuttukkut tuṇiyāka, ap puttakaṅkaḷ maliyāḷa tēcattil acciṭappaṭṭati avarkaḷ cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. maliyāḷa tēcattil acciṭappaṭum puttakam maliyāḷa moḻiyiltāṉ irukkavēṇṭum eṉṉum kaṭṭāyam illi. kiṟittuva matattip paravac ceyta ēcuviṉ capip pātirikaḷiṉ talimi iṭam ak kālattil maliyāḷa tēcattil iruntatu. ēcuviṉ capip pātirikaḷiṉ talivarum maliyāḷa tēcattiltāṉ vāḻntu vantār. ākiyāl, matac cārpāṉa puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭuvataṟkuriya accup poṟikaḷum avviṭattilēyē ēṟpaṭṭiruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil maturiyil vāḻntirunta tattuva pōtaka cuvāmi eṉṉum ēcuviṉ capip pātiriyār tamiḻil eḻutiya “ñāṉōpatēca kāṇṭam” eṉṉum puttakam, tamiḻnāṭṭil akkālattil accup poṟi illātapaṭiyāl, maliyāḷa tēcattil ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil, pātirimārāl niṟuvappaṭṭirunta accuppoṟi niliyattil acciṭappaṭṭateṉṟum apputtakatti acciṟpatippippataṟkākat tamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta antrēppirayā eṉṉum pātiriyār ampalakkāṭṭukkuc ceṉṟār eṉṟum varalāṟṟu vāyilāy aṟikiṟōm. itaṉāl, maliyāḷak kariyil tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum.
ituvaṉṟiyum, mēṟkuṟitta “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” “kiṟittuva vētōpatēcam” eṉṉum iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷum tamiḻp putta kaṅkaḷtām eṉpataṟkuvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avi eṉṉa veṉṟāl, taraṅkampāṭic tiruccapiyic cērnta sārṭṭōriyas pātiriyār11 ceṉṉikku vaṭakkēyuḷḷa paḻavēṟkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil, oru kiṟittuva matapōtakar 1579-il kocciyil acciṭappaṭṭa ‘kiṟittuva vaṇakkam’ eṉṉum tamiḻ accup puttakattip pārtteḻutappaṭṭa kiyeḻuttuppaṭi oṉṟi vittiruntatit tām pārttatāka,1743-il eḻutiyirukkiṟār. mēlum, antap pātiriyār kīḻ varum ceytiyiyum eḻuti yirukkiṟār; kocciyil “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” acciṭappaṭṭa atē accukkūṭattil apputtakam acciṭappaṭṭa atē āṇṭil, ēcuviṉ capip pātiriyārāṉa mārkkas jārj12 eṉpavarāl eḻutap paṭṭu, mēṟpaṭi capiyic cērnta maṟṟoru pātiriyārāṉa āṉriks13 eṉpavarāl tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakattit taraṅkampāṭiyil tām kaṇṭatāka eḻutiyirukkiṟār. itaṉāl anta iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷum tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷē eṉpatu aiyamaṟa viḷaṅkum.
“kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum accup puttakam tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭatu tāṉ eṉpataṟku iṉṉoru cāṉṟum uṇṭu. ṭākṭar pi.jē. tōmas em.ē.,eṉpavar “kēraḷattilē kiṟittīya cākittiyam” eṉṉum puttakatti maliyāḷattil eḻutiyirukkiṟār. avar ap puttakattil “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakattip paṟṟi eḻutiyiruppatu:- “pāris nakarattil pēr peṟṟa sōrpōṉ”14 carva kalācāliyic cērnta puttaka cāliyil ki.pi.1579-il, maliyāḷa tēcattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭa oru vētōpatēca puttakam iruppatāka aṟintēṉ. uṭaṉē, apputtakattiṉ oṉṟiraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷip pōṭṭōp piṭittu aṉuppumpaṭi pātar hōsṟṟaṉ eṉpavar mūlamāka ēṟpāṭu ceytēṉ. appaṭam vantu cērntatum atip pārttapōtu, apputtakam tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷāl acciṭappaṭṭateṉṟu aṟintēṉ. cēra maṇṭalattilum cōḻa maṇṭalattilum upayōkappaṭum poruṭṭu accaṭikkappaṭṭatāka atil eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu koṇṭu, akkālattil iṅku (maliyāḷa tēcattil) tamiḻ moḻikku mutaṉmiyaḷikkappaṭṭiruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu” eṉpatu. itiliruntu 1579-il maliyāḷa tēcattil acciṭappaṭṭa “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakam tamiḻil acciṭappaṭṭa teṉpatum, ataṉ paṭi pāris nakarattil irukkiṟa teṉpatum, viḷaṅkukiṟatu, yām aṟinta variyil, patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil (1550 mutal 1600 variyil) acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ “kiṟittuva vētōpa tēcam,” “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum iraṇṭu nūlkaḷtām. vēṟu cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭirukkalām, āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ vivaram teriya villi.
paṟaṅkiyar kālattil ēcuviṉ capip pātirikaḷiṉ mēṟ pārviyil, teṉ intiyāvil kīḻkkaṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷil accup poṟikaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. ivitām mutaṉmutal intiyāvil niṟuvappaṭṭa accup poṟikaḷ.
maliyāḷa tēcattil ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil  1550-il  oru accup poṟi niṟuvappaṭṭatu.
tirunelvēliyic cērnta puṉṉik kāyal eṉṉum iṭattil, 1578-il, ēcuviṉ capip pātirimār tamiḻ kaṟpataṟkāka oru kallūri niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ik kallūriyiṉ talimi āciriyarāka viruntavar eṉrīkes15 eṉpavar. intak kallūriyiṉ mēṟpārviyil mēṟpaṭi āṇṭil oru accup poṟi amikkappaṭṭatu. vārīyā16 eṉpavar iṅku accu eḻuttukkaḷi uṇṭākkic cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷi acciṟ patippittār. iṅku acciṭappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷ iṉṉavi eṉṟu teriyavilli. ivar uṇṭākkiya accu eḻuttu mara acceḻuttāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. 1578-il “kiṟittuva vētōpatēcam”, “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakaṅkaḷ ivviṭattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kocciyil, 1577-il ēcuviṉ capip pātirimār oru accup poṟi niṟuviṉārkaḷ. aṅku acciṭap paṭṭa tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷiṉ vivaram teriyavilli.
kōvāp paṭṭiṉattil, 1550-il, pōrccukal tēcattiliruntu iraṇṭu accup poṟikaḷ vantaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivi upayōkappaṭuttappaṭāmalē yiruntaṉa. yām aṟinta variyil, 1557-il tāṉ mutaṉ mutal kōvāppaṭṭiṉattil puttakam acciṭappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, apputtakam tamiḻmoḻiyil acciṭap paṭṭataṉṟu. iṅkut tamiḻ puttakaṅkaḷ eppōtāvatu acciṭap paṭṭaṉavā eṉpatum teriyavilli.
maliyāḷa tēcattil cēnta maṅkalattic cērnta aṟuppuk kōṭṭi eṉṉum iṭattil, 1557-il oru tēvālayamum, pātiri kaḷiṉ maṭamum, oru accup poṟiyum niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. kocciyilum itē āṇṭiltāṉ accuppoṟi niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ivviṭattil kōṇsālvas eṉṉum pātiriyār tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷi uṇṭākki, “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakatti acciṭṭatākac collukiṟārkaḷ. intap puttakam tirunelvēliyil uḷḷa puṉṉikkāyal accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatāka vēṟu cilar eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaṉ uṇmi viḷaṅkavilli. ivviṭattil tamiḻmoḻiyilum cila nūlkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vivaram teriyavilli.
maliyāḷa tēcattil, ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil, maṟṟoru poṟi 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil amikkap paṭṭatu. enta āṇṭil niṟuvappaṭṭateṉṟu teriyavilli. āṉāl, 1679-il iṉṉāci āccāmaṇi17 eṉṉum upatēciyār tamiḻil acceḻuttuk kaḷi uṇṭākkiṉār. mēṟpaṭi āṇṭilē antōṉi ṭipril pātiriyār eḻutiya tamiḻ-pōrccukīs akarātiyum, tekōsṟṟā pātiriyār eḻutiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇamum iṅku acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ muḻu vivaramum kiṭikka villi.
paṟaṅkiyar ilaṅkit tīvilum celvākkup peṟṟirun tārkaḷ. aṅku yāḻppāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷil palarik kiṟittavarākkiyum, pala kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiyum mata ūḻiyam ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ yāḻppāṇattil accup poṟikaḷ amittu, tamiḻ nūlkaḷi acciṭṭārkaḷā eṉpatu teriyavilli. yām ārāyntavariyil illi eṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. paṟaṅkiyarukkup piṟaku, intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceyyavanta ollāntar, piṟkālattiṟ paṟaṅkiyarkaḷi intiyāviliruntum ilaṅkiyiliruntum turatti viṭṭu, paṟaṅkiyariṉ āṭciyi aḻittu viṭṭārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, paṟaṅkikaḷiṉ cārpākac camaya ūḻiyam ceytirunta ēcuviṉ capip pātirimārkaḷiyum ūri viṭṭup pōkumpaṭi kaṇṭippāṉa uttaravu iṭṭārkaḷ. ēcuviṉ capip pātirimārkaḷ pōyviṭṭataṉāl avarkaḷ ceytuvanta accu vēlikaḷum niṟuttappaṭṭaṉa. ollāntarum tamatu kiṟittuva matattip parappuvataṟkāka muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. āṉāl, intiyāvil avarkaḷ tamatu vēliyi atikamākac ceyya villi eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ollāntar intiyāvil tamiḻ nūlkaḷi acciṭṭatākat teriyavilli. ollāntar kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil accuvēli niṉṟu viṭṭatu eṉṟu uṟutiyākac collalām. āṉāl, ilaṅkiyil yāḻppāṇattilum, koḷumpilum kalācālikaḷ amittu, kirīkku, lattīṉ, ollānta pāṣi, tamiḻ mutaliya moḻikaḷik kaṟpittu vantārkaḷeṉpatum, oru accup poṟiyum niṟuviṉārkaḷ eṉpatum terikiṉṟaṉa. yāḻppāṇattut tamiḻarākiya mellō pātiriyār18 ollāntaruṭiya kallūriyiṟ kalvi kaṟṟut tērntavarkaḷil oruvar. inta mellō pātiriyār epirēyu, kirīk, ilattīṉ, pōrttukīciyam, ollāntu, tamiḻ eṉṉum āṟu moḻikaḷil vallavar. ivar kirēkka moḻiyiliruntu tamiḻil moḻi peyartta putiya ēṟpāṭu (viviliya vētam) 1749-l ollāntaruṭiya accukkūṭattiṟ patippikkap paṭṭatu. ilaṅkiyil ollāntar kālattil acciṭappaṭṭa vēṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ vivaraṅkaḷ teriyavilli. āṉāl, mēlē colliyapaṭi ollāntar intiyāviliruntu paṟaṅkiyarit turattiviṭṭa piṟaku, kattōlikkak kiṟittavar puttakaṅkaḷi acciṭuvatu niṉṟu viṭṭatu.
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil tañcikkaṭutta taraṅkampāṭiyilirunta ṭēṉiṣ miṣaṉariyāṉa sīkaṉpālku aiyar, tamiḻnāṭṭil accup poṟi illāta kuṟiyippaṟṟic cermaṉi tēcattiluḷḷa tam naṇparkaḷukku eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷiliruntum, ak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭil accuppoṟi oṉṟēṉum iruntirukka villi eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. sīkaṉpālku aiyar eḻutiya kaṭitattil, tamakku vēṇṭiya tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷiyum, pāṭacāli māṇavarukku vēṇṭiya pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷiyum, ōliccuvaṭikaḷil eḻutuvittu varuvataṉāl, atikappaṭiyāṉa kālac celavum, paṇac celavum ākiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, avvitam paṇamum kālamum atikamākac celaviṭṭālum cila paṭikaḷ tām eḻuti muṭikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, ākiyāl paṇac celavik kuṟittut turitamākap puttakaṅkaḷi ērāḷamāka acciṭuvataṟku utaviyākat tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷiyum accup poṟiyiyum aṉuppi vikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tamatu cermaṉi tēcattu naṇparkaḷukku eḻutiyirukkiṟār. ivaratu vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅki, cermaṉi yiliruntu accup poṟiyum, tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷum aṉuppap paṭṭaṉa. ivi 1713-ām āṇṭu taraṅkampāṭikku vantu cērntaṉa. itu tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaṉ mutal amikkappaṭṭa accuppoṟi eṉpatil aiyamilli. itil pala tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. akkālattil acciṭuvataṟkuk kākitam airōppāviliruntu atikamāka varuvatilliyākiyāl, taraṅkampāṭic capiyōr oru kākitap paṭṭari ēṟpaṭuttik kākitam ceytaṉar. āṉāl uḻippukkut takunta payaṉ kiṭikkātapaṭiyāl, attoḻil ciṟitu kālattukkup piṟaku niṟuttappaṭṭatu. ituvē intiyāvil mutaṉ mutal ēṟpaṭṭa kākitat toḻiṟcāli eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. intat taraṅkampāṭi accup poṟi ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku potumakkaḷ accup puttakaṅkaḷi vāṅkip paṭikka atikamāka muṟpaṭṭaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu.
1761-m āṇṭil, iṅkilīṣkārar putuccēriyip piṭittaṉar. appoḻutu āṅkilēyar pireñcuk kavarṉariṉ araṇmaṉiyil irunta ōr accup poṟiyik kippaṟṟiṉar. kippaṟṟiya antap poṟiyic ceṉṉikkuk koṇṭuvantu, vēppēri eṉṉum iṭattil irunta miṣaṉarimāriṉ pārviyil viṭṭuvittaṉar. itu tamiḻ nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭāvatu accup poṟiyākum. itil aracāṅkattārukku vēṇṭiya puttakaṅkaḷum miṣaṉari mārukku vēṇṭiya puttakaṅkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. taraṅkam pāṭic capiyiṉ accukkūṭattiṉāl potu makkaḷukku vēṇṭiya accup puttakaṅkaḷ avvaḷaviyum uṇṭākka muṭiyāmalirunta kuṟi ceṉṉi accup poṟi uṇṭāṉapiṟaku nīṅkiviṭṭatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil inta iraṇṭu accup poṟikaḷ tām iruntaṉa.
āṉāl, 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl, miṣaṉarimār tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ capikaḷi ēṟpaṭutti mummuramāka camayattoṇṭu ceyya muṟpaṭṭārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, muntiya nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷi viṭa inta nūṟṟāṇṭil ērāḷamāṉa pāṭacālikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. intukkaḷum mukammatiyaruṅkūṭat taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ matattip parappuvataṟkākap puttakaṅkaḷip putiyaṉa putiyaṉavāka acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiṉatum inta nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ. aṉṟiyum, ceytikaḷik kūṟum nāḷtāḷ, kiḻamit tāḷkaḷum inta nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. iḵtaṉṟiyum, intiyar accup poṟi vikkak kūṭāteṉṟirunta taṭi 1835-ām āṇṭil nīkkappaṭṭatu. atumutal tamiḻarum accup poṟikaḷ amikka muṟpaṭṭaṉar. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl inta nūṟṟāṇṭil accup puttakaṅkaḷ mikutiyāka acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, kīḻkkaṇṭa accukkūṭaṅkaḷ miṣaṉarimārāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
“es.pi.si.kē.19 eṉṉum caṅkattār ceṉṉiyil 1815-l oru accuppoṟi niṟuviṉārkaḷ.
tirunelvēlip pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyil oru matap piracāra caṅkam 1822-l ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭu, ataṉ cārpāka ōr accuppoṟi niṟuvappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭil nākarkōyil eṉṉum iṭattilum oru capiyum accuppoṟiyum niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa.
1830-l neyyūr eṉṉum iṭattil parappuk kaḻakamu(piracāra capi)m accup poṟiyum amikkappaṭṭaṉa.
1821-l yāḻppāṇattu nallūril carcmicaṉ capiyār ōr accup poṟi niṟuviṉārkaḷ.
1840-l putuccēriyil ōr accuppoṟi niliyam niṟuvappaṭṭatu.
1870-l ilaṅkik kattōlik capiyār ōr accup poṟiyik koḻumpil ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṟaku, iḵtu yāḻppāṇattukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu.
mēṟcoṉṉa accuk kūṭaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷivanta accup puttakaṅkaḷ mikap pala. accupputtakaṅkaḷi uṇṭākkuvataṟku ivi nalla vēli ceytuvantaṉa. iviyaṉṟi, “ceṉṉik kalvic capi,”20 “ceṉṉip pāṭacālip puttakac caṅkam,”21 “teṉṉintiyak kiṟittuvap pāṭacālip puttakac caṅkam”22 mutaliya caṅkaṅkaḷum accup puttakaṅkaḷi veḷippaṭuttuvatil 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē atikamāka uḻittu vantaṉa.

4. viññāṉa nūl varalāṟu
airōppiyariṉ toṭarpiṉāl tamiḻmoḻiyil urinaṭi nūlkaḷum accupputtakaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭatu pōlavē, maṟṟoru vaki nūlum uṇṭāyirukkiṟatu. atutāṉ viññāṉanūl. nām viññāṉa nūl eṉṟu colluvatu pūkōḷa nūl, vāṉa nūl, pirāṇi nūl, kṣēttirak kaṇitam, tēcacarittiram, uṭal nūl mutaliyavi kaḷiyē. airōppiyar varuvataṟku muṉṉē inta viññāṉa nūlkaḷ (vāṉa nūlit tavira) tamiḻ moḻiyilum ēṉiya intiya moḻikaḷilum iruntaṉavilli. eviyēṉum iruntaṉaveṉṟāl. avi uṇmikku māṟāṉa kaṟpaṉik katikaḷākattāṉ iruntirukkakkūṭum. pāl, tayir, ney, karuppañcāṟu mutalāṉa eḻuvakip poruḷkaḷāl nirampappeṟṟa ēḻukaṭalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, cakaraṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ tōṇṭiyapaṭiyāl cākaram (kaṭal) uṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatum, malikaḷ paṇṭik kālattiṟ ciṟakukaḷip peṟṟu ākāyattil iṅkum aṅkum viruppampōl paṟantu tirintu koṇṭiruntapōtu avaṟṟiṉ ciṟakukaḷi intiraṉ veṭṭi vīḻttiyapaṭiyāl avi paṟakkamuṭiyāmal pūmiyilē viḻuntu nakara muṭiyāmal aṅkaṅkē kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, pūmiyip pāypōl curuṭṭik koṇṭupōy ōr acuraṉ kaṭalukkuḷ oḷittu vittāṉ eṉpatum, ivi pōṉṟa kaṟpaṉik katikaḷumē nammuṭiya paṇṭip pūkōḷa nūlkaḷ. pūmiyic cūriyaṉ cuṟṟi varukiṟa teṉpatum, irāku kētu eṉṉum pāmpukaḷ cūriya cantirarkaḷi oru paki kāraṇamāka viḻuṅkuvatāl cūriya cantira kiraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, takkaṉuṭiya cāpattiṉāl cantiraṉ tēyvatum civaṉuṭiya aruḷiṉāl atu vaḷarvatum nēriṭukiṟa teṉpatum, ivi pōṉṟavikaḷumē namatu paṇṭikkāla vāṉa cāttiraṅkaḷ. mēkam kaṭalil mēyntu taṇṇīrik kuṭittu vāṉattiṟ ceṉṟu maḻiyākap peykiṟa teṉpatum, intiraṉuṭiya kōpat tiṉāl iṭi miṉṉalkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, ivi pōṉṟa kaṟpaṉikaḷumē paṇṭikkāla iyaṟkic cāttiraṅkaḷ. ittakiya kaṟpaṉik katikaḷip paṭittum kēṭṭum paṇṭik kāla makkaḷ uṇmiyuṇarāmal mūṭattaṉattil āḻntiruntaṉar. airōppiyariṉ toṭarpu uṇṭāṉa piṟaku, pātirimārkaḷ pāṭacālikaḷi amittu, namatu nāṭṭuc ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷukku airōppiya vaḻakkattip piṉpaṟṟip pāṭaṅkaḷip pōtikka muṟpaṭṭapoḻutu, eḻutal, paṭittal, kaṇakkuppōṭutal eṉṉum mūṉṟuṭaṉ maṭṭum nillāmal, pūkōḷa nūl, vāṉa nūl, iyaṟkip poruḷ nūl, kṣēttirak kaṇitam, tēcacarittiram mutaliya viññāṉa nūlkaḷiyum kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. pāṭacālikaḷil ittakiya viññāṉa nūlkaḷip pōtikkat toṭaṅkiyapaṭiyāl, tamiḻil innūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭiya avaciyam ēṟpaṭṭatu. mutalil pātiri mārum micaṉarimārum, piṉṉar aracāṅkattārum viññāṉa nūlkaḷit tamiḻil eḻuti acciṭṭum piṟari eḻutumpaṭi tūṇṭiyum icceykiyil pērutavi ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ.
inta viññāṉa nūlkaḷum ceṉṟa pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilētāṉ tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. innūlkaḷiṉ muḻuvaralāṟṟiṉiyum kūṟuvataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ muṟṟum emakkuk kiṭikkavilli. kiṭitta cilavaṟṟik koṇṭu oruvāṟu kūṟa muṟpaṭukiṉṟōm.
“ceṉṉip pāṭacālip puttakac caṅkam” eṉṉum ic caṅkam ēṟpaṭṭu, pāṭacālip puttakaṅkaḷiyum vēṟu puttakaṅkaḷiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭu vantatu. iccaṅkam 1850-mutal atikac cuṟucuṟuppāka vēliceyyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iccaṅkam matac cārpaṟṟa potuvāṉa caṅkam. pāṭacālip puttakaṅkaḷi acciṭṭu utaviyatōṭu pūkōḷam, carittiram mutaliya viññāṉa nūlkaḷiyum tamiḻil eḻuti veḷippaṭuttiyatu. tamiḻil putiya nūlkaḷi eḻutuvōrukku naṉkoṭiyaḷittu ūkkap paṭuttiyatu iccaṅkam. “intutēca carittiram,” “ulaka carittiram,” “pala tēca carittiram,” “ irāpiṉcaṉ kurūcō” mutalāṉa nūlkaḷ iccaṅkattiṉ ūkkattiṉāl veḷivantavi.
itit tavira, kiṟittuva matac cārpāka, “teṉṉintiyak kiṟittuvap pāṭacālip puttakac caṅkam”1 eṉṉum caṅkam 1854-l ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. iṉṉum, pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi, nākarkōyil, neyyūr, yāḻppāṇam mutaliya ūrkaḷilum kiṟittuva micaṉarimār, ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil accukkūṭaṅkaḷi niṟuvi, viññāṉa nūlkaḷiyum ēṉiya puttakaṅkaḷiyum acciṭṭu veḷippaṭutti yirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta viññāṉa nūlkaḷi yām aṟintavariyil kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
pūkōḷa nūl
pūmi cāttiram: iti irēṉiyus aiyar 1832-il eḻutic ceṉṉiyil acciṭṭār. itutāṉ mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa pūkōḷa nūl eṉat terikiṟatu. katri2 eṉpavar eḻutiya “ārampap pūkōḷam”3 eṉṉum nūli ātāramākak koṇṭu ivar inta nūli eḻutiṉār.
pūmi cāttirac curukkam (1846), pūmicāttirak kuṟippu. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷum nākarkōyil accukkūṭattiṟ patippikkap paṭṭaṉa. pūmi cāttirap poḻippu ilaṅkiyilum, pūmi cāttirap pāṭaṅkaḷ putuccēriyilum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. iviyaṉṟi, iṉṉum anēka pūmi cāttira nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ērāḷamāka veḷiyiṭap paṭṭaṉa.
tēca carittirak katikaḷ
ulaka carittiram (1881), iṅkilāntu tēca carittiram (1858), intu tēca carittiram, pala tēca carittirac curukkam, urōma carittiram,4 ivi ceṉṉip pāṭacālip puttakac caṅkattārāl veḷiyiṭap paṭṭavi.
piriṭṭiṣ tēca carittiram (1855),5 purātaṉac carittirac carukkam,6 (1851) ivviru puttakaṅkaḷum revaraṇṭu hāps eṉpavarāl eḻutip pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi accuk kūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.
ulaka carittira māli,7 (1830) itu ṭākṭar ṣimiṭ8 eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭuc ceṉṉi ci.em.es. accuk kūṭattiṟ patippikkap paṭṭatu.
cātāraṇa itikācam9: iḵtu ārṉālṭ10 eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭu, 1858-il yāḻppāṇattu amerikkaṉ micaṉ accuk kūṭattiṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu. pūrvika carittiram11 revaraṇṭu sārjaṇṭ eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭa ipputtakam 1850-il pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
intu cāti viḷakkam cārls cimaṉ eṉpavarāl eḻuti 1852-il ceṉṉiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam mutaliyaṉa
vīca kaṇitam12: itu yāḻppāṇattavarāṉa kāral13 eṉpavarāl 1855-il eḻutappaṭṭu, yāḻppāṇattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam: itu pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭic carc micaṉ uyartarap pāṭacāliyiṉ talimiyāciriyarāyirunta ṭēviṭ cālamaṉ eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭatu. 1859-il acciṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam14 āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum eḻutap paṭṭa in nūl 1899-il ceṉṉiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu. ec. hālim, em.ṭi. aṭikkalam eṉpavarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭatu.
iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷ15 kaṉam ji.yu. pōp aiyaravarkaḷāl acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi yārāl tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu teriyavilli.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam16: itu sprāṭ17 eṉpavarāl pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
nila aḷavi nūl18: iḵtu 1858-il acciṭappaṭṭatu. moḻi peyarppāḷar iṉṉār eṉṟu teriyavilli.
iyaṟkip poruḷ nūl, pirāṇi nūl mutaliyaṉa
palavakit tiruṭṭāntam19: itu potu aṟivu nūl. kaṉam irēṉiyus aiyarāl eḻutip pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
ūrtiri vilaṅkiyal (1836-varucam)20, vaṉavilaṅkiyal21 maccaviyal: ivi nākarkōyil accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.
niyāya ilakkaṇam: intat tarkka cāttiram yāḻp pāṇattavarāṉa villiyam neviṉs22 eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭu, yāḻppāṇattil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
aṅkātipātam23: kirīṉ24 eṉpavar eḻutiyatu; 1872-il yāḻppāṇattil acciṭappaṭṭatu. arumiyāṉa ciṟanta nūl.
maruntuc carakkukaḷiṉ peyar25: ituvum kirīṉ eṉpavar eḻutiyatu; 1875-il nākarkōyil accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
iracāyaṉa mutal nūl26 pavar aiyarāl eḻutappaṭṭa racāyaṉa nūl. kemistam27: kirīṉ vittiyar iyaṟṟiyatu. 1875-ām āṇṭil nākarkōyilil acciṭappaṭṭatu. vāṉa cāttiram, cukaraṇavātam, uṟpālaṉam eṉṉum nūlkaḷum yāḻppāṇattil acciṭap paṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa viññāṉa nūlkaḷiṉ muḻu vivaramum emakkuk kiṭikkātapaṭiyāl, emakkut terintavariyil curukkamāka eḻutappaṭṭatu.

5. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum airōppiyat 
ticic coṟkaḷ
vaḻakkāṟṟiluḷḷa ellā moḻikaḷilum ayal nāṭṭut ticic coṟkaḷ kalantuviṭuvatu iyaṟki. iḵtu ekkālattilum ellā nāṭṭilum ellā moḻikaḷilum ēṟpaṭṭuvarukiṟatu. ki.mu.1015-ām āṇṭil irunta cālamaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ kālattilum, avaṉukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattilum, tuṇi, tantam, kuraṅku, iñci, miḷaku, arici, mayil, cantaṉakkaṭṭi mutaliya poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tamiḻp peyarākavē epirēya1 moḻiyil vaḻaṅki vantati “pipiḷ” eṉṉum viviliya nūliṟ kāṇalām. avvitamē, arici, iñcivēr pōṉṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kirīkku, ilattīṉ eṉṉum moḻikaḷiṟ citintu vaḻaṅki vantatiyum kāṇkiṟōm. paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē intiyāvukku vanta āriyar pēciya āriyamoḻiyilum tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ pala kalantirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycci valla aṟivāḷarkaḷ collukiṟārkaḷ. āṅkila moḻiyilum kaṟi, kaṭṭumaram, miḷakuttaṇṇīr, kūli, veṟṟili mutaliya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ citintu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟē airōppiyat ticic coṟkaḷ cila tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ippōtu āṅkilam aracāṅka moḻiyāka iruppatu paṟṟiyum, āṅkilam kaṟṟavarkkē aluval kiṭikkiṉṟami paṟṟiyum, vaṇikat tuṟiyilum āṅkilamoḻi vēṇṭappaṭukiṟatu paṟṟiyum nām āṅkilam kaṟṟuvarukiṟōm. ivvitam āṅkilat toṭar puṭimipaṟṟic cila āṅkilac coṟkaḷ tamiḻil, mutaṉmiyākap pēccuvaḻakkiṟ payiṉṟuvarukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟik kīḻē tarukiṟōm: kavarṉar, kōrṭṭu, āpīs, kāppi, riṭṭar, paṭlar, jaṭj, pēppar, piṉalkōṭu, cammaṉ, cāppu, mōṭṭār, ṣarṭṭu, kōṭṭu, kampeṉi, carccu, micaṉ, rōṭṭu, kamiṭṭi, mīṭṭiṅku, ṭirāmkār, cōppu mutaliyaṉa. itu niṟka.
16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal namatu nāṭṭil vāṇikam ceytuvanta pōrccukīciyar, ollāntar, pireñcukkārar mutaliya airōppiya iṉattāriṉ moḻic coṟkaḷum tamiḻiṟ kalantirukkavēṇṭu mallavā? itippaṟṟi ārāyumiṭattup pōrccukīciya rākiya paṟaṅkiyar moḻic coṟkaḷ tavira, ēṉiya ollāntu moḻic coṟkaḷum pireñcumoḻic coṟkaḷum tamiḻiṟ kalantu vaḻaṅkiyatākat teriyavilli. pireñcu, ollāntu, ṭēṉiṣ moḻic coṟkaḷmaṭṭum tamiḻiṟ kalakkāmal, pōrccukīcu moḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum tamiḻiṟ kalantatu putumiyākat tōṉṟalām. 16,17,18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil namatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil nāṉku airōppiya iṉattār vantiruntu vāṇikam ceytirukka, annāṉku moḻic coṟkaḷum tamiḻil ticic coṟkaḷākak kalakkāmal, pōrccukīcu moḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum kalakkavēṇṭiya kāraṇam eṉṉa? kāraṇam ituvākum: mutaṉmutalil namatu nāṭṭiṟku vanta airōppiya iṉattār pōrccukīciyarāvar. avarkaḷukkup piṟakutāṉ maṟṟa airōppiyac cātiyār iṅku vantaṉar. mutalil vanta pōrccukīcuc cātiyār namatu nāṭṭiṉiṟ cilarik kiṟittavarākki, avarkaḷukkup pōrccukīcu moḻiyik kaṟpittaṉar. aṉṟiyum, ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōṭṭi kaḷik kāppataṟkāka aḻittuvanta cippāykaḷukku intiyap peṇ kaḷik kaliyāṇam ceytuvittaṉar. intak kalappu maṇattiṉāṟ piṟanta piḷḷikaḷ ‘tuppācikaḷ’ eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa oru taṉi iṉattārāka iruntaṉar. intat tuppācikaḷ pōrccukīcu moḻiyit taṅkaḷ tāymoḻiyākap pēcivantaṉar. (ippoḻutu intiyar kaḷukkum āṅkilēyarukkum piṟanta piḷḷikaḷ ‘āṅkilō intiyar’ eṉṉum peyarpeṟṟu, āṅkilamoḻiyit tāymoḻi yākak koṇṭiruppatupōla, pōrccukīciyarukkum intiyarukkum piṟanta tuppāci eṉṉum vakuppār pōrccukīcu moḻiyit tāymoḻiyākap pēcivantaṉar.) pōrccukīciyarukkup piṟaku namatu nāṭṭiṟku vanta maṟṟa airōppiyarum pōrccukīcu moḻiyiyē akkālattil intiyāvil potumoḻiyākap pēcivantārkaḷ. airōppiya varttakar ellōrum pōrccukīcu moḻiyip potumoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkivantatupaṟṟi, vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum piṟa kāraṇattiṉporuṭṭum avarkaḷuṭaṉ paḻakiya tamiḻarum ammoḻiyi akkālattiṟ kaṟkavēṇṭiyatu kaṭṭāyamāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka namatu nāṭṭiṟ pōrccukīcu moḻi oruvakiyiṟ potumoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkivantapaṭiyāl, ammoḻic coṟkaḷmaṭṭum tamiḻiṟ kalakka, maṟṟa airōppiya moḻic coṟkaḷ kalakka iṭamillātapaṭi pōyiṉa. pōrccukīcu moḻic coṟkaḷ cila tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvataṟku ituvē kāraṇamākum. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum pōrccukīcut ticiccoṟkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉavi eṉpati ārāyntu yāḻppāṇattu nallūr uyartiru. ñāṉappirakāca cuvāmikaḷ, O.M.I., āṅkilap pattirikiyoṉṟil2 oru kaṭṭuri eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. cuvāmikaḷiṉ uttaravu peṟṟu akkaṭṭuriyiliruntu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum paṟaṅkic coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟik kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
aṉṉāci allatu aṉṉatāḻi, koyyā, pappāy (pappaḷi), vāttu, kuciṉi, pīṅkāṉ, kōppi, vicukkōttu,3 arakku,4 kamicu, cappāttu, pāppūcu, pottāṉ, aluppunētti, mēci, katiri5 vāṅku6puṉal7, kaṭutāci, pēṉā, kirāti, virānti,8 cimēntu9 pīppā, mēstiri, kappittāṉ, āyā (cevilittāy)10 pātiri, vicuppu11 pāppu12 paṭṭāḷam, turuppu, cippāy, tuppākki, vayiṉāttu,13 kumpācu, naṅkūram, cāvi, ēlam, racītu, ācupattiri, iskūl, kumpātiri,14 kummātiri,15 viviliyam,16 paṭṭācu, caṉṉal mutaliyaṉa.
uyartiru.ñāṉappirakāca cuvāmikaḷ ārāynteḻutiya paṟaṅkic coṟkaḷ attaṉiyum iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭavilli. avarkaḷ eḻutiyavaṟṟil oru cilavaṟṟimaṭṭum mēlē kuṟippiṭṭōm. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ellāp paṟaṅkic coṟkaḷiyum aṟiya virumpuvōr mēlē coṉṉa āṅkilappattirikiyip pārttu aṟintu koḷḷalām. ap paṟaṅkit ticiccoṟkaḷiṟ cila maṭṭum ellāt tamiḻarkaḷālum vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila coṟkaḷ kiṟittuvat tamiḻarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟuñ cila ilaṅkit tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil ippōtu vaḻak koḻintaṉavum cila uṇṭu.
